{"parentUuid":"1f52b88b-f7f2-4191-a764-54222a354d01","isSidechain":false,"attachment":{"type":"mcp_instructions_delta","addedNames":["plugin:context7:context7","plugin:serena:serena"],"addedBlocks":["## plugin:context7:context7\nUse this server to fetch current documentation whenever the user asks about a library, framework, SDK, API, CLI tool, or cloud service -- even well-known ones like React, Next.js, Prisma, Express, Tailwind, Django, or Spring Boot. This includes API syntax, configuration, version migration, library-specific debugging, setup instructions, and CLI tool usage. Use even when you think you know the answer -- your training data may not reflect recent changes. Prefer this over web search for library docs.\n\nDo not use for: refactoring, writing scripts from scratch, debugging business logic, code review, or general programming concepts.","## plugin:serena:serena\nCRITICAL: Before starting to work on a coding task, call the `initial_instructions` tool to read the 'Serena Instructions Manual'."],"removedNames":[]},"type":"attachment","uuid":"39ce1dcd-f88e-4b6a-96c9-35a66bf87b22","timestamp":"2026-04-29T13:06:24.071Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"45ff2d8f-d633-45f8-8a6e-c5fb5ce08842","version":"2.1.122","gitBranch":"main"}
{"parentUuid":"39ce1dcd-f88e-4b6a-96c9-35a66bf87b22","isSidechain":false,"attachment":{"type":"skill_listing","content":"- update-config: Use this skill to configure the Claude Code harness via settings.json. Automated behaviors (\"from now on when X\", \"each time X\", \"whenever X\", \"before/after X\") require hooks configured in settings.json - the harness executes these, not Claude, so memory/preferences cannot fulfill them. Also use for: permissions (\"allow X\", \"add permission\", \"move permission to\"), env vars (\"set X=Y\"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: \"allow npm commands\", \"add bq permission to global settings\", \"move permission to user settings\", \"set DEBUG=true\", \"when claude stops show X\". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.\n- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: \"rebind ctrl+s\", \"add a chord shortcut\", \"change the submit key\", \"customize keybindings\".\n- simplify: Review changed code for reuse, quality, and efficiency, then fix any issues found.\n- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.\n- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo). Omit the interval to let the model self-pace. - When the user wants to set up a recurring task, poll for status, or run something repeatedly on an interval (e.g. \"check the deploy every 5 minutes\", \"keep running /babysit-prs\"). Do NOT invoke for one-off tasks.\n- schedule: Create, update, list, or run scheduled remote agents (routines) that execute on a cron schedule. - When the user wants to schedule a recurring remote agent, set up automated tasks, create a cron job for Claude Code, or manage their scheduled agents/routines. Also use when the user wants a one-time scheduled run (\"run this once at 3pm\", \"remind me to check X tomorrow\").\n- claude-api: Build, debug, and optimize Claude API / Anthropic SDK apps. Apps built with this skill should include prompt caching. Also handles migrating existing Claude API code between Claude model versions (4.5 → 4.6, 4.6 → 4.7, retired-model replacements).\nTRIGGER when: code imports `anthropic`/`@anthropic-ai/sdk`; user asks for the Claude API, Anthropic SDK, or Managed Agents; user adds/modifies/tunes a Claude feature (caching, thinking, compaction, tool use, batch, files, citations, memory) or model (Opus/Sonnet/Haiku) in a file; questions about prompt caching / cache hit rate in an Anthropic SDK project.\nSKIP: file imports `openai`/other-provider SDK, filename like `*-openai.py`/`*-generic.py`, provider-neutral code, general programming/ML.\n- ccs-delegation: Auto-activate CCS CLI delegation for deterministic tasks. Parses user input, auto-selects optimal profile (glm/kimi/custom) from ~/.ccs/config.json, enhances prompts with context, executes via `ccs {profile} -p \"task\"` or `ccs {profile}:continue`, and reports results. Triggers on \"use ccs [task]\" patterns, typo/test/refactor keywords. Excludes complex architecture, security-critical code, performance optimization, breaking changes.\n- attach: Port the Claude Code harness layer from this project to a new project directory. Use when bootstrapping a fresh Claude Code project on the same machine, when peers want to copy the harness, or when verifying layer separation via dry-run. Reads .claude/manifest.yaml; copies harness-tagged artifacts only; excludes stockforge biz + personal layers; generates skeleton CLAUDE.md + manifest at target.\n- crawler-reliability: Build reliable web crawlers for data gathering. Use when implementing scrapers for CafeF, NDH, VietnamBiz, YouTube (yt-dlp), Facebook fanpages, or any external data source. Covers Python/Playwright patterns, retry/backoff, selector robustness, rate limiting, monitoring.\n- ddd-tactical-patterns: Apply DDD tactical patterns (aggregate, entity, value object, repository, domain event, anti-corruption layer) correctly in Python/dataclasses code. Use when designing or implementing domain models, repositories, or cross-bounded-context communication in StockForge's 9 BCs.\n- decompose-work: Split a non-trivial task into deterministic-vs-LLM portions before execution. Use when planning multi-step work where some sub-tasks are mechanical (file ops, regex, count, schema validation) and others require judgment (synthesis, design, ambiguity resolution). Outputs structured plan that maps each portion to the cheapest competent tool. Pairs with capability-map.md (model × effort × task_class grounding) and try-n-approaches (S9 — Karpathy autoresearch loop).\n- evidence-extraction: Extract structured claims from unstructured sources with citation integrity. Use when building extraction pipelines, parsing news articles or KOL transcripts, or anywhere claims are created from raw data. Ensures every claim has source_url, extracted_at, confidence, and verified attribution. Enforces no-LLM-math invariant (I-S1).\n- fastapi-module: Implement FastAPI routers following StockForge conventions. Use when creating a new HTTP endpoint, adding routes, wiring dependency injection, configuring Pydantic DTOs. Covers router boundaries, provider patterns, testing setup. Phase 2+ only — Phase 1 uses Streamlit dashboard directly.\n- grill-maximization: Doctrine for bundling Q&A questions to the human at maximum density per turn (15-20 target, max 25), so each human-in-the-loop touchpoint is information-rich. Use when composing a Q&A bundle for human-workspace/q-and-a/pending/, or when deciding whether to ask one question or wait and bundle. Built on the principle that human-in-the-loop time is the scarcest resource in autonomous coding.\n- obsidian-vault: Search, create, and manage notes in the StockForge Obsidian vault with wikilinks, frontmatter, and index maintenance. Use when adding content to the knowledge base, navigating entities/concepts, or maintaining the raw/wiki separation. Adapted from mattpocock/skills, MIT licensed. Extended with raw/wiki pattern from Karpathy LLM Wiki.\n- postgres-pgvector: Design Postgres schemas with pgvector and TimescaleDB for StockForge. Use when creating migrations, designing tables, writing queries. Covers vector embeddings for semantic search on Vietnamese text, time-series for price/sentiment data, JSONB vs structured columns.\n- promote-rule: Cluster agent-notes.md entries by lexical similarity, propose promotion of recurring rules to deterministic hook (priority 1), skill (priority 2), or charter (priority 3). Use when agent-notes has accumulated ≥10 new rules since last promotion run, or on-demand at phase boundary. Outputs proposal markdown to observations/. Pairs with capability-map.md (writes new task_class entries) and decompose-work (consumes promotion outcomes).\n- prompt-engineering: Design and optimize LLM prompts for StockForge pipeline stages. Use when writing or refining prompts for claim extraction, KOL recommendation extraction, sentiment analysis, thesis synthesis. Covers context assembly, prompt caching strategy, budget-aware prompts, structured output. Enforces no-LLM-math constraint.\n- qa-escalation: File-based Q&A protocol — composes bundle file in human-workspace/q-and-a/pending/, tracks lifecycle (pending → answered → stale → processed), creates URGENT notification when needed. Use when intent classifier recommends OPEN_QA_BUNDLE / ESCALATE_HUMAN, or grill-maximization has a bundle ready to write. Pairs with grill-maximization (composes content) + Track 5 hooks (auto-move stale).\n- spec-dual-layer: Write specifications with dual-layer structure (business narrative + agent contract). Use when authoring new specs, refining existing specs, or understanding the spec template. Ensures both human stakeholder needs and agent execution needs are served.\n- spec-to-wiki: Convert raw specifications to Obsidian wiki format with wikilinks, frontmatter, and index updates. Use when adding a new spec to the knowledge base, or when regenerating wiki view after spec changes. Maintains raw as source of truth, wiki as navigable view.\n- test-pyramid-balance: Balance unit, integration, and E2E tests properly in Python/pytest. Use when deciding what test to write, reviewing test coverage, or rebalancing skewed test suite. Covers test pyramid shape, BDD vs unit tests, when to use what. Includes stock-domain-specific test categories.\n- ubiquitous-language: Extract a DDD-style ubiquitous language glossary from the current conversation or domain area. Use when starting work on a new bounded context, when new domain terms emerge, or when verifying consistency between business language and code. Adapted from mattpocock/skills, MIT licensed.\n- user-prompt-intake: Hybrid intent classifier — main session lite-detects trivial prompts, dispatches intent-classifier subagent for complex ones. Use whenever a new user prompt lands (chat or file in human-workspace/user_prompt/) and you need to route the work. Built for full-autonomous loop where every prompt must be classified before action.\n- write-a-skill: Create new Claude Code skills with proper structure, progressive disclosure, and discoverability. Use when extracting a pattern from multiple sessions into a reusable skill, or when user asks to formalize a technique. Adapted from mattpocock/skills, MIT licensed.\n- ccs:continue: Continue last CCS delegation session [AUTO ENHANCE]\n- ccs: Delegate task with intelligent profile selection [AUTO ENHANCE]\n- budget-check: /budget-check — Current Session Token Budget Status\n- devils-advocate: /devils-advocate — Adversarial Critique\n- drift-check: /drift-check — Run Drift Signals DR1-DR12\n- drill-me: /drill-me — Interactive Ubiquitous Language Extraction\n- grill-me: /grill-me — Relentless Plan/Design Interview\n- handoff-read: /handoff-read — Lightweight Last Session Load\n- master-plan: /master-plan — High-Level Work Decomposition\n- session-end: /session-end — Close Work Session\n- session-start: /session-start — Begin Work Session\n- session-verify: /session-verify — Mid-Session Alignment Check\n- spec-author: /spec-author — Create Dual-Layer Specification\n- spec-to-wiki: /spec-to-wiki — Convert Raw Spec to Obsidian Wiki Format\n- ul-audit: /ul-audit — Audit Ubiquitous Language Consistency\n- vbw-check: /vbw-check — Apply Verify-Before-Write Protocol\n- init: Initialize a new CLAUDE.md file with codebase documentation\n- review: Review a pull request\n- security-review: Complete a security review of the pending changes on the current branch","skillCount":45,"isInitial":true},"type":"attachment","uuid":"ee8ca494-a719-4be5-b8ba-a36753ef2a0c","timestamp":"2026-04-29T13:06:24.071Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"45ff2d8f-d633-45f8-8a6e-c5fb5ce08842","version":"2.1.122","gitBranch":"main"}

consider firing AskUserQuestion in this session\"\n# Do NOT auto-fire — surface to agent who decides timing\n```\n\nThis keeps the queue auditable + actionable without LLM-Guardian continuous polling.\n","numLines":157,"startLine":1,"totalLines":157}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"7ba8b6b9-da47-41ce-85a9-b01841b42790","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"45ff2d8f-d633-45f8-8a6e-c5fb5ce08842","version":"2.1.122","gitBranch":"main"}

{"parentUuid":"35b904f2-646c-4fb7-a41a-139e719f87fd","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"fc11436c-fada-41bc-8ae7-32ac3f984211","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01EHTTNufLGN6hixsb2TrwwT","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t# PROJECT CHARTER\n2\t## StockForge — Personal Investment Advisor for Vietnamese Stock Market\n3\t\n4\t> **Status**: Immutable v1.0 — changes require explicit charter revision.\n5\t> **Scope of immutability**: Vision, principles, success criteria.\n6\t> **Things that do evolve**: Agent configs, specs, implementation, signal weights, eval sets.\n7\t\n8\t---\n9\t\n10\t## Vision\n11\t\n12\tAn AI-first investment research and advisory system that combines **quant fund methodology** with **Vietnam-specific edge sources** (KOL networks, crowd sentiment, pump pattern detection) to surface high-conviction opportunities in an inefficient market where logic-based screening alone is insufficient.\n13\t\n14\tPrimary user: project owner (self) + 3-5 trusted peers in Vietnamese investing community.\n15\t\n16\tPrimary goal: build a system that compounds edge over time through proprietary data accumulation, not through any single clever algorithm. After 12-24 months of dogfood, the system should be a genuine advisor I would pay money for if someone else built it.\n17\t\n18\tNot a goal: high-frequency trading, replacing human judgment, becoming a SaaS in Year 1, beating frontier LLMs at general reasoning, providing licensed financial advice.\n19\t\n20\t---\n21\t\n22\t## The Core Insight (the why)\n23\t\n24\t**Quant funds (Renaissance, Two Sigma, DE Shaw) win in efficient markets through clean data + execution speed + diverse alpha factors.** They don't use sentiment from Facebook/YouTube because at their scale it's noise, not signal — and in efficient markets it gets arbitraged away anyway.\n25\t\n26\t**Vietnam stock market inverts this.** It is structurally inefficient:\n27\t- 85-90% retail investor share (vs 20-25% in US)\n28\t- Information asymmetry between sophisticated and retail crowd\n29\t- KOL/influencer recommendations measurably move prices\n30\t- \"Đội lái\" (pump operators) operate openly\n31\t- News and narrative cycles dominate over fundamentals for months at a time\n32\t- Foreign flow creates non-fundamental price pressure\n33\t\n34\tIn this market, **sentiment + influence + narrative tracking is signal, not noise**. And LLMs can process Vietnamese unstructured text at scale that was impossible before 2023.\n35\t\n36\tThis creates a **structural arbitrage**: apply quant methodology + LLM reasoning + sentiment/influence data layers that no commercial product offers for VN. Position not as \"decision maker\" but as **advisor + alert watcher + bias mirror**.\n37\t\n38\t---\n39\t\n40\t## The Craft Philosophy\n41\t\n42\tTake the craft seriously. Build for personal use first, commercial second.\n43\t\n44\t- Production-grade engineering standards from Day 1 (DDD + clean architecture + multi-loop discipline)\n45\t- Commercial extension kept as option, never as driver\n46\t- When self-use conflicts with hypothetical-future-SaaS, self-use wins\n47\t- Quality bar: I genuinely trust output enough to act on real money decisions\n48\t- Edge comes from compounding moats (proprietary data, calibration, personal bias log), not from any single feature\n49\t\n50\tReference models: Renaissance Technologies (data + iteration discipline), Bridgewater (principles-based decision making), personal Bloomberg Terminal (data density + analysis depth), Athletic.com (subscription-quality content for niche audience).\n51\t\n52\t---\n53\t\n54\t## Core Principles (non-negotiable)\n55\t\n56\t1. **Evidence grounding** — every claim, every number traceable to source + as-of date. Hallucination in finance = bug, not stylistic issue.\n57\t\n58\t2. **Structured output over narrative** — multi-criteria assessment, never single \"buy/sell\" score. Output is trade-off matrix.\n59\t\n60\t3. **Adversarial by design** — bear + bull + critic + quant + behavior + manager perspectives. Never consensus from single agent.\n61\t\n62\t4. **Proprietary data moat** — every news ingested, every KOL recommendation tracked, every thesis post-mortem'd compounds the edge. Raw data is sacred.\n63\t\n64\t5. **Pattern transfer + local adaptation** — global setups (cyclical bottom, dividend value, GARP) + Vietnam-specific overlays (policy timing, foreign flow, narrative phase).\n65\t\n66\t6. **Human-in-loop is the product** — augment thinking, never replace it. System should challenge user's gut instinct without overriding it.\n67\t\n68\t7. **Dogfood mandatory** — if I don't use it weekly for real decisions, the feature gets killed. Self-use is the only validation that matters in Year 1.\n69\t\n70\t8. **Calibration over confidence** — system tracks its own accuracy across signal types, never claims confidence it hasn't earned through hit rate.\n71\t\n72\t9. **No LLM math** — LLM never generates numbers. All calculations through deterministic code with verified data inputs. LLM only interprets.\n73\t\n74\t10. **Position sizing & risk management are deterministic** — code-enforced rules, LLM cannot override. Maximum position size, sector concentration, stop loss rules are immutable per session.\n75\t\n76\t---\n77\t\n78\t## The Four-Tier Signal Architecture\n79\t\n80\tSystem operates on four distinct signal tiers, each with different lead time, reliability, and processing approach:\n81\t\n82\t**Tier 1 — Hard Data (deterministic):** prices, volumes, fundamentals, foreign flow, macro indicators. Zero LLM, pure code. Lead time: hours-days.\n83\t\n84\t**Tier 2 — Official Narrative (semi-structured):** mainstream financial news, broker reports, official company disclosures, government announcements. LLM extracts claims + classifies sentiment + detects contradictions. Lead time: real-time.\n85\t\n86\t**Tier 3 — Influence Network (semi-structured):** YouTube channels, Facebook KOL pages, Telegram channels, podcasts. LLM transcribes + extracts recommendations + tags entities. Calibration database tracks each KOL's accuracy over time. Lead time: can lead by days.\n87\t\n88\t**Tier 4 — Crowd Sentiment & Pump Detection (unstructured):** community forums, public chat groups, comments. LLM classifies sentiment + detects coordinated posting + identifies pump narratives + tracks narrative lifecycle phase. Lead time: typically lags price (signals exhaustion).\n89\t\n90\tThe combination — when Tier 1+2+3 agree before Tier 4 heats up = early opportunity; when Tier 4 hot but Tier 1+2 don't support = likely top — is the core analytical edge.\n91\t\n92\tSee `specs/tier1-strategic/001-four-tier-signal-architecture.md` for detailed tier specs.\n93\t\n94\t---\n95\t\n96\t## First Sub-Scope (locked 6 months)\n97\t\n98\t**Vietnam stocks listed on HOSE/HNX/UPCoM**, specifically:\n99\t- VN30 + mid-cap (vốn hóa 2,000-50,000 tỷ VND) for primary coverage\n100\t- Long-only equity strategies (no derivatives, no shorting)\n101\t- Holding periods: 1-month minimum, 6-24 month preferred\n102\t- Position sizing per stock: 5-15% of portfolio\n103\t\n104\tOut of scope (Year 1): derivatives, warrants (CW), short selling, cryptocurrency, fund products, foreign markets, day trading, automated execution.\n105\t\n106\t---\n107\t\n108\t## Honest Boundaries (what system does NOT do)\n109\t\n110\t- **Predict price** — system predicts narrative phase, money flow direction, mispricing level. Never a price target with false precision.\n111\t- **Replace fundamental research** — surfaces opportunities, user must verify thesis before acting.\n112\t- **Execute trades** — pure research/alert tool. Execution stays manual.\n113\t- **Provide licensed financial advice** — personal research aid only. Output explicitly framed as \"thesis exploration\", not \"buy/sell recommendation\".\n114\t- **Time the market precisely** — works on phase identification (early/mid/late), not on tick-level timing.\n115\t- **Insider information** — uses only public sources. No paid leaks, no insider channels, no anything that crosses legal/ethical lines.\n116\t\n117\t---\n118\t\n119\t## Success Criteria\n120\t\n121\t**Month 3 (end of Phase 1 — Foundation):**\n122\t- Tier 1 + 2 data pipeline operational for VN30\n123\t- 50 companies have basic dossier in wiki\n124\t- First 5 thesis recorded with formal structure\n125\t- Can run /thesis-validate on a stock end-to-end in <5 minutes\n126\t\n127\t**Month 6 (end of Phase 2 — Edge Sources):**\n128\t- 30+ KOL channels tracked, calibration database has >100 recommendations with outcomes pending\n129\t- Tier 3 + 4 ingestion operational for top 100 stocks\n130\t- First detectable pump pattern caught (warning issued before public dump)\n131\t- 20+ thesis logged, first 5 hit 3-month review\n132\t\n133\t**Month 9 (end of Phase 3 — Multi-perspective):**\n134\t- Bear/bull/critic/quant/macro/behavior agents operational\n135\t- Pattern library has 15+ Vietnam-specific patterns with outcome data\n136\t- Personal bias log shows 3+ identified biases\n137\t- Thesis hit rate measurable (statistically meaningful sample)\n138\t\n139\t**Month 12 (end of Phase 4 — Compounding):**\n140\t- Karpathy outer loop optimizing signal weights weekly\n141\t- Cross-validation: system suggestions vs my manual analysis = where do they diverge?\n142\t- 3-5 trusted peers using it, providing feedback\n143\t- Demonstrable alpha vs VN-Index on dogfood portfolio (or honest accounting of why not)\n144\t\n145\t**Ultimate signal of success**: When my gut says \"buy XYZ\", I check what StockForge says first — and seriously consider it when it disagrees.\n146\t\n147\t---\n148\t\n149\t## Technical Foundation (locked Phase 1-3)\n150\t\n151\t**Stack:**\n152\t- **Python primary** — for data science, LLM workflows, time-series, backtesting (heavier than IdeaForge which was TS-primary)\n153\t- **Postgres 16 + TimescaleDB + pgvector + pg_trgm** — single DB for time-series + relational + vector\n154\t- **Redis** — cache + pub/sub + queue broker\n155\t- **Cloudflare R2** — raw content storage (PDFs, transcripts, scraped pages)\n156\t- **Claude API primary** — Sonnet for most tasks, Opus for thesis synthesis\n157\t- **OpenAI embeddings** — text-embedding-3-small (Vietnamese acceptable)\n158\t- **NestJS** — only if/when we need a public-facing API (Phase 4+)\n159\t- **Streamlit/Next.js** — Streamlit for personal dashboard (Phase 1-2), Next.js if SaaS path\n160\t- **Apache Airflow / Prefect** — data pipeline orchestration\n161\t\n162\t**Architecture:**\n163\t- Clean Architecture with DDD tactical patterns (same as IdeaForge)\n164\t- 9 bounded contexts (see `agent-workspace/constitution/architecture.md`)\n165\t- Event-driven for cross-context communication\n166\t- Multi-loop execution (Karpathy outer + Continuous middle + Per-stock inner)\n167\t\n168\t**Development approach:**\n169\t- AI-first: Claude Code as primary builder, human as spec author + reviewer + bias-checker\n170\t- Spec-Driven (SDD) + Domain-Driven (DDD) + selective BDD\n171\t- Sandwich pattern (Architect → Dev → Verifier) from Day 1\n172\t- Verify-Before-Write (VBW) protocol mandatory\n173\t- Three-tier quality gates (deterministic / probabilistic / human)\n174\t\n175\t---\n176\t\n177\t## Data Sources (Tier 1 budget)\n178\t\n179\t**Free/cheap (Phase 1):**\n180\t- vnstock (open source) — historical EOD, fundamentals\n181\t- Vietstock public — financial reports\n182\t- TCBS API — semi-public market data\n183\t- CafeF, NDH, VietnamBiz — news scraping\n184\t- Public YouTube channels — transcript via yt-dlp + Whisper\n185\t- Public Facebook fanpages — selective scraping (legal grey, careful scope)\n186\t\n187\t**Paid (Phase 2-3 if budget allows):**\n188\t- FiinPro Platform — comprehensive fundamental data (~30-100M VND/year)\n189\t- Wichart — pre-cleaned data\n190\t- SSI FastConnect — realtime quotes if needed\n191\t\n192\t**Budget cap Year 1**: 50M VND for data + LLM combined.\n193\t\n194\t---\n195\t\n196\t## Meta-Discipline\n197\t\n198\tThese charter principles override daily decisions:\n199\t\n200\t- **When in doubt, simplify** — minimum code that solves the problem, nothing speculative\n201\t- **Ship thin slices, not perfect systems** — each phase ends with something I actually use\n202\t- **Document what you learn, not what you plan** — post-mortem > prospective docs\n203\t- **Dogfood or delete** — features I don't use weekly get removed\n204\t- **Calibrate, don't guess** — every confidence claim must have outcome tracking\n205\t- **Stay agnostic about commercial outcome** — build for the craft, not for the exit\n206\t\n207\t---\n208\t\n209\t## Revision Protocol\n210\t\n211\tThis charter changes only when:\n212\t- A core principle proves materially wrong through 30+ hours of real use\n213\t- Scope demonstrably needs revision based on accumulated dogfood data\n214\t- Technical foundation invalidated by external event (tool deprecation, regulatory change, etc.)\n215\t\n216\tCharter revisions require:\n217\t- Written rationale with evidence (linked to specific sessions/post-mortems)\n218\t- 48-hour cool-down before committing change\n219\t- Explicit version bump (v1.0 → v2.0)\n220\t\n221\tMinor clarifications go in `agent-workspace/memory/agent-notes.md`. This charter stays stable.\n222\t\n223\t---\n224\t\n225\t## Document Index\n226\t\n227\t| Document | Purpose | Stability |\n228\t|---|---|---|\n229\t| `PROJECT_CHARTER.md` (this) | Vision + principles | Immutable for ~3 months |\n230\t| `AGENT_OPERATING_MANUAL.md` | Agent configs, skills, workflows | Living, frequent updates |\n231\t| `SPEC_TEMPLATE.md` | How to write specs | Stable |\n232\t| `specs/tier1-strategic/001-four-tier-signal-architecture.md` | Strategic: signal architecture | Stable |\n233\t| `specs/tier2-feature/001-validate-investment-thesis.md` | First implementation spec | Stable |\n234\t| `specs/tier2-feature/002-influence-network-tracking.md` | KOL tracking system spec | Stable |\n235\t| `specs/tier2-feature/003-crowd-sentiment-pump-detection.md` | Pump detection spec | Stable |\n236\t| `specs/tier2-feature/004-multi-perspective-adversarial-agents.md` | Adversarial analysis spec | Stable |\n237\t| `specs/tier2-feature/005-karpathy-outer-loop.md` | Pipeline self-optimization spec | Stable |\n238\t| `agent-workspace/memory/project.md` | Current state | Continuously updated |\n239\t| `agent-workspace/memory/agent-notes.md` | Learned rules | Append-mostly |\n240\t| `agent-workspace/memory/thesis-log/` | All thesis ever recorded | Append-only |\n241\t| `agent-workspace/memory/post-mortems/` | Thesis outcome reviews | Append-only |\n242\t| `agent-workspace/calibration/` | KOL accuracy, signal calibration data | Continuously updated |\n243\t\n244\t---\n245\t\n246\t## Anti-Charter (what we explicitly reject)\n247\t\n248\tTo stay focused, we reject these tempting paths:\n249\t\n250\t- **Predict prices with ML models** — models trained on VN price data overfit; no edge there.\n251\t- **Compete with broker research** — we have less data, less staff. We win on integration + edge sources, not on raw analysis depth.\n252\t- **Build for general retail public** — we build for ourselves and 3-5 peers. Mass market needs hand-holding we won't provide.\n253\t- **Trust LLM for numbers** — every numerical output must come from code with traceable inputs.\n254\t- **Build features without dogfooding** — speculative features die fast in this project.\n255\t- **Optimize for backtest performance** — Goodhart's Law is real. We optimize for forward decision quality.\n256\t- **Make money fast** — this is a 24-month project minimum to reach edge. Quick wins are anti-pattern.\n257\t"}]},"uuid":"f90ca8cb-66b7-4190-8088-2d2e394d5b8b","timestamp":"2026-04-29T13:06:36.520Z","toolUseResult":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\PROJECT_CHARTER.md","content":"# PROJECT CHARTER\n## StockForge — Personal Investment Advisor for Vietnamese Stock Market\n\n> **Status**: Immutable v1.0 — changes require explicit charter revision.\n> **Scope of immutability**: Vision, principles, success criteria.\n> **Things that do evolve**: Agent configs, specs, implementation, signal weights, eval sets.\n\n---\n\n## Vision\n\nAn AI-first investment research and advisory system that combines **quant fund methodology** with **Vietnam-specific edge sources** (KOL networks, crowd sentiment, pump pattern detection) to surface high-conviction opportunities in an inefficient market where logic-based screening alone is insufficient.\n\nPrimary user: project owner (self) + 3-5 trusted peers in Vietnamese investing community.\n\nPrimary goal: build a system that compounds edge over time through proprietary data accumulation, not through any single clever algorithm. After 12-24 months of dogfood, the system should be a genuine advisor I would pay money for if someone else built it.\n\nNot a goal: high-frequency trading, replacing human judgment, becoming a SaaS in Year 1, beating frontier LLMs at general reasoning, providing licensed financial advice.\n\n---\n\n## The Core Insight (the why)\n\n**Quant funds (Renaissance, Two Sigma, DE Shaw) win in efficient markets through clean data + execution speed + diverse alpha factors.** They don't use sentiment from Facebook/YouTube because at their scale it's noise, not signal — and in efficient markets it gets arbitraged away anyway.\n\n**Vietnam stock market inverts this.** It is structurally inefficient:\n- 85-90% retail investor share (vs 20-25% in US)\n- Information asymmetry between sophisticated and retail crowd\n- KOL/influencer recommendations measurably move prices\n- \"Đội lái\" (pump operators) operate openly\n- News and narrative cycles dominate over fundamentals for months at a time\n- Foreign flow creates non-fundamental price pressure\n\nIn this market, **sentiment + influence + narrative tracking is signal, not noise**. And LLMs can process Vietnamese unstructured text at scale that was impossible before 2023.\n\nThis creates a **structural arbitrage**: apply quant methodology + LLM reasoning + sentiment/influence data layers that no commercial product offers for VN. Position not as \"decision maker\" but as **advisor + alert watcher + bias mirror**.\n\n---\n\n## The Craft Philosophy\n\nTake the craft seriously. Build for personal use first, commercial second.\n\n- Production-grade engineering standards from Day 1 (DDD + clean architecture + multi-loop discipline)\n- Commercial extension kept as option, never as driver\n- When self-use conflicts with hypothetical-future-SaaS, self-use wins\n- Quality bar: I genuinely trust output enough to act on real money decisions\n- Edge comes from compounding moats (proprietary data, calibration, personal bias log), not from any single feature\n\nReference models: Renaissance Technologies (data + iteration discipline), Bridgewater (principles-based decision making), personal Bloomberg Terminal (data density + analysis depth), Athletic.com (subscription-quality content for niche audience).\n\n---\n\n## Core Principles (non-negotiable)\n\n1. **Evidence grounding** — every claim, every number traceable to source + as-of date. Hallucination in finance = bug, not stylistic issue.\n\n2. **Structured output over narrative** — multi-criteria assessment, never single \"buy/sell\" score. Output is trade-off matrix.\n\n3. **Adversarial by design** — bear + bull + critic + quant + behavior + manager perspectives. Never consensus from single agent.\n\n4. **Proprietary data moat** — every news ingested, every KOL recommendation tracked, every thesis post-mortem'd compounds the edge. Raw data is sacred.\n\n5. **Pattern transfer + local adaptation** — global setups (cyclical bottom, dividend value, GARP) + Vietnam-specific overlays (policy timing, foreign flow, narrative phase).\n\n6. **Human-in-loop is the product** — augment thinking, never replace it. System should challenge user's gut instinct without overriding it.\n\n7. **Dogfood mandatory** — if I don't use it weekly for real decisions, the feature gets killed. Self-use is the only validation that matters in Year 1.\n\n8. **Calibration over confidence** — system tracks its own accuracy across signal types, never claims confidence it hasn't earned through hit rate.\n\n9. **No LLM math** — LLM never generates numbers. All calculations through deterministic code with verified data inputs. LLM only interprets.\n\n10. **Position sizing & risk management are deterministic** — code-enforced rules, LLM cannot override. Maximum position size, sector concentration, stop loss rules are immutable per session.\n\n---\n\n## The Four-Tier Signal Architecture\n\nSystem operates on four distinct signal tiers, each with different lead time, reliability, and processing approach:\n\n**Tier 1 — Hard Data (deterministic):** prices, volumes, fundamentals, foreign flow, macro indicators. Zero LLM, pure code. Lead time: hours-days.\n\n**Tier 2 — Official Narrative (semi-structured):** mainstream financial news, broker reports, official company disclosures, government announcements. LLM extracts claims + classifies sentiment + detects contradictions. Lead time: real-time.\n\n**Tier 3 — Influence Network (semi-structured):** YouTube channels, Facebook KOL pages, Telegram channels, podcasts. LLM transcribes + extracts recommendations + tags entities. Calibration database tracks each KOL's accuracy over time. Lead time: can lead by days.\n\n**Tier 4 — Crowd Sentiment & Pump Detection (unstructured):** community forums, public chat groups, comments. LLM classifies sentiment + detects coordinated posting + identifies pump narratives + tracks narrative lifecycle phase. Lead time: typically lags price (signals exhaustion).\n\nThe combination — when Tier 1+2+3 agree before Tier 4 heats up = early opportunity; when Tier 4 hot but Tier 1+2 don't support = likely top — is the core analytical edge.\n\nSee `specs/tier1-strategic/001-four-tier-signal-architecture.md` for detailed tier specs.\n\n---\n\n## First Sub-Scope (locked 6 months)\n\n**Vietnam stocks listed on HOSE/HNX/UPCoM**, specifically:\n- VN30 + mid-cap (vốn hóa 2,000-50,000 tỷ VND) for primary coverage\n- Long-only equity strategies (no derivatives, no shorting)\n- Holding periods: 1-month minimum, 6-24 month preferred\n- Position sizing per stock: 5-15% of portfolio\n\nOut of scope (Year 1): derivatives, warrants (CW), short selling, cryptocurrency, fund products, foreign markets, day trading, automated execution.\n\n---\n\n## Honest Boundaries (what system does NOT do)\n\n- **Predict price** — system predicts narrative phase, money flow direction, mispricing level. Never a price target with false precision.\n- **Replace fundamental research** — surfaces opportunities, user must verify thesis before acting.\n- **Execute trades** — pure research/alert tool. Execution stays manual.\n- **Provide licensed financial advice** — personal research aid only. Output explicitly framed as \"thesis exploration\", not \"buy/sell recommendation\".\n- **Time the market precisely** — works on phase identification (early/mid/late), not on tick-level timing.\n- **Insider information** — uses only public sources. No paid leaks, no insider channels, no anything that crosses legal/ethical lines.\n\n---\n\n## Success Criteria\n\n**Month 3 (end of Phase 1 — Foundation):**\n- Tier 1 + 2 data pipeline operational for VN30\n- 50 companies have basic dossier in wiki\n- First 5 thesis recorded with formal structure\n- Can run /thesis-validate on a stock end-to-end in <5 minutes\n\n**Month 6 (end of Phase 2 — Edge Sources):**\n- 30+ KOL channels tracked, calibration database has >100 recommendations with outcomes pending\n- Tier 3 + 4 ingestion operational for top 100 stocks\n- First detectable pump pattern caught (warning issued before public dump)\n- 20+ thesis logged, first 5 hit 3-month review\n\n**Month 9 (end of Phase 3 — Multi-perspective):**\n- Bear/bull/critic/quant/macro/behavior agents operational\n- Pattern library has 15+ Vietnam-specific patterns with outcome data\n- Personal bias log shows 3+ identified biases\n- Thesis hit rate measurable (statistically meaningful sample)\n\n**Month 12 (end of Phase 4 — Compounding):**\n- Karpathy outer loop optimizing signal weights weekly\n- Cross-validation: system suggestions vs my manual analysis = where do they diverge?\n- 3-5 trusted peers using it, providing feedback\n- Demonstrable alpha vs VN-Index on dogfood portfolio (or honest accounting of why not)\n\n**Ultimate signal of success**: When my gut says \"buy XYZ\", I check what StockForge says first — and seriously consider it when it disagrees.\n\n---\n\n## Technical Foundation (locked Phase 1-3)\n\n**Stack:**\n- **Python primary** — for data science, LLM workflows, time-series, backtesting (heavier than IdeaForge which was TS-primary)\n- **Postgres 16 + TimescaleDB + pgvector + pg_trgm** — single DB for time-series + relational + vector\n- **Redis** — cache + pub/sub + queue broker\n- **Cloudflare R2** — raw content storage (PDFs, transcripts, scraped pages)\n- **Claude API primary** — Sonnet for most tasks, Opus for thesis synthesis\n- **OpenAI embeddings** — text-embedding-3-small (Vietnamese acceptable)\n- **NestJS** — only if/when we need a public-facing API (Phase 4+)\n- **Streamlit/Next.js** — Streamlit for personal dashboard (Phase 1-2), Next.js if SaaS path\n- **Apache Airflow / Prefect** — data pipeline orchestration\n\n**Architecture:**\n- Clean Architecture with DDD tactical patterns (same as IdeaForge)\n- 9 bounded contexts (see `agent-workspace/constitution/architecture.md`)\n- Event-driven for cross-context communication\n- Multi-loop execution (Karpathy outer + Continuous middle + Per-stock inner)\n\n**Development approach:**\n- AI-first: Claude Code as primary builder, human as spec author + reviewer + bias-checker\n- Spec-Driven (SDD) + Domain-Driven (DDD) + selective BDD\n- Sandwich pattern (Architect → Dev → Verifier) from Day 1\n- Verify-Before-Write (VBW) protocol mandatory\n- Three-tier quality gates (deterministic / probabilistic / human)\n\n---\n\n## Data Sources (Tier 1 budget)\n\n**Free/cheap (Phase 1):**\n- vnstock (open source) — historical EOD, fundamentals\n- Vietstock public — financial reports\n- TCBS API — semi-public market data\n- CafeF, NDH, VietnamBiz — news scraping\n- Public YouTube channels — transcript via yt-dlp + Whisper\n- Public Facebook fanpages — selective scraping (legal grey, careful scope)\n\n**Paid (Phase 2-3 if budget allows):**\n- FiinPro Platform — comprehensive fundamental data (~30-100M VND/year)\n- Wichart — pre-cleaned data\n- SSI FastConnect — realtime quotes if needed\n\n**Budget cap Year 1**: 50M VND for data + LLM combined.\n\n---\n\n## Meta-Discipline\n\nThese charter principles override daily decisions:\n\n- **When in doubt, simplify** — minimum code that solves the problem, nothing speculative\n- **Ship thin slices, not perfect systems** — each phase ends with something I actually use\n- **Document what you learn, not what you plan** — post-mortem > prospective docs\n- **Dogfood or delete** — features I don't use weekly get removed\n- **Calibrate, don't guess** — every confidence claim must have outcome tracking\n- **Stay agnostic about commercial outcome** — build for the craft, not for the exit\n\n---\n\n## Revision Protocol\n\nThis charter changes only when:\n- A core principle proves materially wrong through 30+ hours of real use\n- Scope demonstrably needs revision based on accumulated dogfood data\n- Technical foundation invalidated by external event (tool deprecation, regulatory change, etc.)\n\nCharter revisions require:\n- Written rationale with evidence (linked to specific sessions/post-mortems)\n- 48-hour cool-down before committing change\n- Explicit version bump (v1.0 → v2.0)\n\nMinor clarifications go in `agent-workspace/memory/agent-notes.md`. This charter stays stable.\n\n---\n\n## Document Index\n\n| Document | Purpose | Stability |\n|---|---|---|\n| `PROJECT_CHARTER.md` (this) | Vision + principles | Immutable for ~3 months |\n| `AGENT_OPERATING_MANUAL.md` | Agent configs, skills, workflows | Living, frequent updates |\n| `SPEC_TEMPLATE.md` | How to write specs | Stable |\n| `specs/tier1-strategic/001-four-tier-signal-architecture.md` | Strategic: signal architecture | Stable |\n| `specs/tier2-feature/001-validate-investment-thesis.md` | First implementation spec | Stable |\n| `specs/tier2-feature/002-influence-network-tracking.md` | KOL tracking system spec | Stable |\n| `specs/tier2-feature/003-crowd-sentiment-pump-detection.md` | Pump detection spec | Stable |\n| `specs/tier2-feature/004-multi-perspective-adversarial-agents.md` | Adversarial analysis spec | Stable |\n| `specs/tier2-feature/005-karpathy-outer-loop.md` | Pipeline self-optimization spec | Stable |\n| `agent-workspace/memory/project.md` | Current state | Continuously updated |\n| `agent-workspace/memory/agent-notes.md` | Learned rules | Append-mostly |\n| `agent-workspace/memory/thesis-log/` | All thesis ever recorded | Append-only |\n| `agent-workspace/memory/post-mortems/` | Thesis outcome reviews | Append-only |\n| `agent-workspace/calibration/` | KOL accuracy, signal calibration data | Continuously updated |\n\n---\n\n## Anti-Charter (what we explicitly reject)\n\nTo stay focused, we reject these tempting paths:\n\n- **Predict prices with ML models** — models trained on VN price data overfit; no edge there.\n- **Compete with broker research** — we have less data, less staff. We win on integration + edge sources, not on raw analysis depth.\n- **Build for general retail public** — we build for ourselves and 3-5 peers. Mass market needs hand-holding we won't provide.\n- **Trust LLM for numbers** — every numerical output must come from code with traceable inputs.\n- **Build features without dogfooding** — speculative features die fast in this project.\n- **Optimize for backtest performance** — Goodhart's Law is real. We optimize for forward decision quality.\n- **Make money fast** — this is a 24-month project minimum to reach edge. Quick wins are anti-pattern.\n","numLines":257,"startLine":1,"totalLines":257}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"35b904f2-646c-4fb7-a41a-139e719f87fd","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"45ff2d8f-d633-45f8-8a6e-c5fb5ce08842","version":"2.1.122","gitBranch":"main"}

{"parentUuid":"9c1b0b19-c8a1-4211-8dbf-acfdb5e3cf4f","isSidechain":false,"attachment":{"type":"nested_memory","path":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\agent-workspace\\CLAUDE.md","content":{"path":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\agent-workspace\\CLAUDE.md","type":"Project","content":"# agent-workspace/ — Contract\n\n> **Audience**: agents working inside `agent-workspace/`.\n> **Established**: Decision 002 § Track 1 (Workspace Dualism Foundation).\n\n## Identity\n\n`agent-workspace/` is the **agent-owned execution + memory** layer. Everything here represents **agent state, decisions, plans, observations, learning**. Agents WRITE here freely (within constitution rules); humans READ here but normally do not edit.\n\nThis separation exists to prevent two failure modes from orch:\n1. **Charter drift via shared-workspace mutation** (orch CF-DOGFOOD-2)\n2. **Human cognitive overload from dense session logs** (UP02 §1.3 — \"human đối mặt hàng chục spec/document quá tải nếu không sắp xếp khoa học\")\n\nThe agent owns this directory but must keep it AUDITABLE — every artifact provenance-linked, every decision sourced.\n\n---\n\n## Subdirectories\n\n| Path | Purpose | Lifecycle |\n|---|---|---|\n| `constitution/` | **IMMUTABLE without explicit user approval**. Charter-tier rules (autonomous-protocol, model-routing, identity-scope, etc.). Track 7 produces drafts in `proposals/`; user explicit approve to move here. | Edit denied by .claude/settings.json; Write only via human approval gate |\n| `memory/project.md` | High-level project state; phase tracker; ADRs (last 5). Update on phase boundaries + architectural decisions. | Long-lived; trim to most recent decisions |\n| `memory/current-execution.md` | **THE single routing source of truth**. Active phase + active session + autonomous_mode flag + routing table. Read FIRST every session. | Long-lived; updated each session boundary |\n| `memory/agent-notes.md` | Learned rules from real experience (post-mortem / drift / user correction). Append-only mostly. | Long-lived |\n| `memory/decisions/` | Sequential ADRs (`NNN-<slug>.md`) + `_template.md` + `README.md` index. 12-field schema (Track 2). | Append-only; supersession via status field |\n| `memory/sessions/` | One file per session: `YYYY-MM-DD-session-N.md`. What happened, decisions made, files touched. | Append-only |\n| `memory/observations/` | Subagent return artifacts (per Track 6 spec). Each subagent writes one observation file describing what it did. | Append; cleaned periodically by aggregator |\n| `memory/checkpoints/` | Session handoff state for self-reboot (Track 5). `latest.md` is canonical pointer; timestamped historical files retained. | Append + latest.md updated each checkpoint |\n| `memory/patterns-discovered/` | Pattern mining outputs (Track 0 — DONE) + ongoing pattern catalog. | Append; significant patterns promoted to constitution |\n| `memory/drift-logs/` | Drift-check (DR1-DR12) results; runs on hook + on-demand. | Time-series append |\n| `memory/post-mortems/` | After significant failure or thesis-revoked event: what failed, root cause, prevention rule. | Append |\n| `memory/thesis-log/` | Stock-domain thesis exploration entries. Read-only for IMPL sessions. | Append + revisited per calibration |\n| `memory/sync-tracker/` | Track 8a output: Confidence Score SQLite + weights.yaml + auto-rendered `_index.md`. | Live updated by hooks |\n| `memory/self-awareness/` | Track 9 output: model × effort × thinking profile cards + known-issues + best-practices. | Live updated by Stop-hook aggregator |\n| `memory/mistake-log.md` | Track 7 deliverable: structured failure catalog. What went wrong / Root cause / Prevention rule / Severity. | Append; pre-flight read by all agents |\n| `session-plans/pending/` | Plans authored, awaiting execution. Phase 0 plan lives here. | Move to `completed/` when done |\n| `session-plans/completed/` | Executed plans; archived for reference + retrospective input. | Append |\n| `quality-reports/{deterministic,probabilistic,drift-reports}/` | Tier-1/2/3 gate outputs (CLAUDE.md § Quality Gates). | Per-run files |\n| `ubiquitous-language/` | DDD glossary + domain-mapping + drift-log. Stockforge stock-domain vocabulary. | Append; canonical via `/drill-me` |\n| `calibration/` | Empirical hit-rate data per signal type. Confidence claims MUST trace here (CLAUDE.md hard rule). | Append per thesis post-mortem |\n| `research/` | Research notes from `research-scanner` subagent dispatches (e.g., reference-repo studies). | Append |\n\n---\n\n## Contract Rules (BINDING)\n\n1. **Constitution is immutable absent explicit human approval.** `Edit/Write` to `agent-workspace/constitution/**` is in `.claude/settings.json` deny list. Track 7 writes constitution drafts to `proposals/`; user explicit approve moves them.\n\n2. **`memory/decisions/` is append-mostly + supersession-status.** Don't delete decisions; mark `status: superseded-by-D-NNN` to revoke. Sequential numbering (`001`, `002`, ...) — don't reuse numbers.\n\n3. **`current-execution.md` is the routing source-of-truth.** No skill, agent, or command may hardcode phase paths or session paths from memory; resolve via this file. Stale paths in CLAUDE.md or skills = anti-pattern.\n\n4. **Never edit `obsidian-vault/raw/`.** Immutable. Vault writes only to `wiki/`. (Established in stockforge CLAUDE.md.)\n\n5. **Provenance is mandatory.** Every decision file references its source (human prompt / spec clause / drift detection / agent inference). Every thesis cites source + as-of date (I-S2). Every confidence claim cites historical hit rate (I-S20-equivalent).\n\n6. **Agents never `git commit` in this directory unless user explicitly requests.** Stage only.\n\n7. **`patterns-discovered/` artifacts are READ-ONLY for production code.** Patterns must be promoted to constitution / skills / hook scripts before becoming binding. (Otherwise they're informal notes, not contracts.)\n\n---\n\n## Reading Priority for Agent\n\nPer `agent-workspace/constitution/autonomous-protocol.md` (Track 7) and stockforge `CLAUDE.md` § Session Protocol:\n\n1. **`memory/current-execution.md`** — first; resolve active track\n2. **`memory/project.md`** — project state\n3. **`memory/checkpoints/latest.md`** if a recent checkpoint exists (within last 24h) — resume context\n4. **`memory/agent-notes.md`** — learned rules\n5. **`memory/mistake-log.md`** — pre-flight failure catalog\n6. **Last 3 files in `memory/sessions/`** — recent context\n7. **`session-plans/pending/<active-plan>.md`** — current plan\n8. **Relevant constitution files** as task demands (don't load all every session)\n9. **Relevant skills + agents + commands** as task demands\n\n---\n\n## Anti-Patterns to Avoid\n\n(From `memory/patterns-discovered/SYNTHESIS.md` § 6, full catalog 23 items. Highest-priority for this directory:)\n\n- **AP-1 Same-agent self-review** — never review your own implementation; dispatch fresh-context verifier\n- **AP-2 Self-track wind-down** — `.transcript-tokens` real-transcript is authoritative, not LLM self-track\n- **AP-5 Charter-coherence defer overriding user-CRITICAL** — re-read all `user_prompt/*` at every phase entry; never silently defer USER-CRITICAL\n- **AP-7 Performative SC ticking** — defer with explicit prerequisites > fire vacuous proposals\n- **AP-8 Pre-staged work causing checkpoint drift** — update `current-execution.md` immediately on task complete, not session-end\n- **AP-17 Identity drift** — stockforge is AI-first VN stock advisory; not generic financial framework (see `constitution/identity-scope.md` once Track 7 ships)\n- **AP-23 Continuous LLM-Guardian** — deterministic hooks = Guardian; LLM Guardian only at session-end aggregation\n\n---\n\n## Connection to human-workspace/\n\nThis directory is the agent's domain. Human's domain is `human-workspace/`. UP-08 delta = +22-23% (under 25% trigger, but material; surfaced explicitly per UP-06 NO Silent File-Defaults).\"\n214\t  budget_before: \"~2.02M (D-003 REV-2)\"\n215\t  budget_after: \"~2.44M (D-005 REV-3 = D-003 + 5.5d ~420K)\"\n216\t  delta_percent: \"+22-23%\"\n217\t  delta_absolute: \"+~420K\"\n218\t  user_acceptance: \"AskUserQuestion Round 2 Q5=A explicit pick at 2026-04-29T18:10:00Z\"\n219\t---\n220\t\n221\t# Decision 005 — UP-08 Track 5.5d Insertion: Self-Learning Pipeline as Write-Heavy Data-ETL\n222\t\n223\t> **Status**: ACCEPTED 2026-04-29 via 5 explicit AskUserQuestion picks (4 SCOPE Round 1 + 1 budget Round 2).\n224\t> **Extends**: D-003 (Track 5.5 master amendment) by adding sibling sub-track 5.5d alongside 5.5a/b/c.\n225\t> **Q&A audit**: `human-workspace/q-and-a/pending/2026-04-29-006-up08-self-learning-pipeline.md`.\n226\t> **Decomposition**: `agent-workspace/memory/observations/decompose-work-up08-S9.md`.\n227\t\n228\t---\n229\t\n230\t## Context\n231\t\n232\tUP-08 (`human-workspace/user_prompt/20260429_08.txt`) raises a foundational distinction the existing\n233\tplan didn't articulate: **runtime is read-heavy + reasoning + delegating; self-learning/upgrade is\n234\twrite-heavy + indexing + caching**. They are different disciplines with different data shapes,\n235\tdifferent cost profiles, and different boundaries. Conflating them in shared storage causes runtime\n236\tto load write-only data accidentally (size + irrelevance penalty).\n237\t\n238\tUser-provided framing: **\"harness engineering = backend engineering / data ETL\"**. This reframes\n239\tself-learning from a feature into a sub-system with its own boundary, queue topology, indexing\n240\tstrategy, and background-processing rules.\n241\t\n242\t### Audit at decision time (S9 PLAN, post-S8 close)\n243\t\n244\t**5.5c primitives shipped S8 (DONE — necessary but not sufficient for UP-08)**:\n245\t- `decompose-work` skill (deterministic vs LLM split)\n246\t- `capability-map.md` (model × effort × task_class grounding)\n247\t- `promote-rule` skill (agent-notes → hook/skill/charter promotion path)\n248\t- `correction-rate-aggregator.sh` (cron-via-Stop-hook digest pattern)\n249\t- 5.5c.5 JSONL telemetry (component-telemetry.sh — pattern to extend)\n250\t- S8 capability-map L-1..L-12 (limits to respect, esp. L-1 NO LLM math + L-3 verification)\n251\t\n252\t**Missing from 5.5c (to ship in 5.5d)**:\n253\t- Data-boundary discipline (separate FS path + drift signal D9)\n254\t- Queue rotation + retention infrastructure (sweeper hook)\n255\t- Index rebuild trigger (RAG-style query target — SQLite FTS Phase 0; pgvector Phase 1+)\n256\t- Periodic background-dispatch rule (event-count or N-session threshold)\n257\t- Karpathy outer loop framing (try-n-approaches consumer; first dogfood)\n258\t- Opensource-tool research arm (research-scanner agent-pick-1 + dogfood)\n259\t\n260\t---\n261\t\n262\t## Decision\n263\t\n264\t**Insert Track 5.5d as new sub-track parallel to 5.5a/b/c, executed across 3 sub-sessions\n265\t(5.5d.1 + 5.5d.2 + 5.5d.3) in S10/S11/S12, before remaining 5.5c.3+4+5 (now S13) and Track 6\n266\tREWRITE (now S14).**\n267\t\n268\t### Sub-track 5.5d.1 — Boundary + Collection Foundation (~120K, 1 session = S10)\n269\t\n270\t**Goals**:\n271\t- Define data-boundary FS layout: `agent-workspace/learning-data/` (write-only), with subdirs `events/` (NDJSON), `index/` (RAG), `archive/` (rotated).\n272\t- Author `agent-workspace/learning-data/README.md` documenting boundary discipline (read path MUST NOT load this tree).\n273\t- Extend `.gitignore` for `learning-data/` (events/archive gitignored; index/ tracked optionally).\n274\t- Extend `scripts/hooks/component-telemetry.sh` to emit learning events to `learning-data/events/YYYY-MM-DD.ndjson` (append-only NDJSON).\n275\t- Add D9 drift signal to `scripts/hooks/drift-signals-D1-D8.sh` (rename to D1-D9): flags any runtime read-path code that loads `learning-data/`.\n276\t- `.claude/settings.json` permissions: deny direct read of `learning-data/events/` from main session (write-only via hook); allow `learning-data/index/` read (RAG query path).\n277\t\n278\t**Files affected**:\n279\t- NEW `agent-workspace/learning-data/README.md`\n280\t- NEW `agent-workspace/learning-data/.gitkeep` files for events/index/archive subdirs\n281\t- MODIFIED `.gitignore` (+5 lines)\n282\t- MODIFIED `scripts/hooks/component-telemetry.sh` (+15-30 LOC: emit learning event after existing telemetry block)\n283\t- MODIFIED `scripts/hooks/drift-signals-D1-D8.sh` → renamed `drift-signals-D1-D9.sh` (+25 LOC: D9 runtime-path-leak signal + script rename + caller updates)\n284\t- MODIFIED `.claude/settings.json` (permissions block)\n285\t\n286\t**Success**:\n287\t- `learning-data/` tree exists; permissions block runtime read of `events/`\n288\t- `component-telemetry.sh` smoke-test emits learning event line; NDJSON parses\n289\t- D9 drift signal detects 0 violations on current codebase (boundary clean from start)\n290\t- `git check-ignore agent-workspace/learning-data/events/test.ndjson` returns PASS\n291\t\n292\t### Sub-track 5.5d.2 — Sweeper + Index + First Analysis (~150K, 1 session = S11)\n293\t\n294\t**Goals**:\n295\t- Author `scripts/hooks/learning-queue-sweeper.sh` (~80 LOC): rotate >7-day events to `archive/`; delete >30-day archive; enforce size guards.\n296\t- Author `scripts/hooks/learning-index-rebuild.sh` (~60-100 LOC): when N events queued (deterministic threshold) → rebuild index. Phase 0: SQLite FTS5 on event NDJSON. Phase 1+: swap to pgvector with embedding emitter.\n297\t- Wire both hooks: `learning-queue-sweeper.sh` to SessionStart hook (cron-style); `learning-index-rebuild.sh` to Stop hook with event-count gate.\n298\t- Background-dispatch trigger rule (H-2 from decomposition): event-count threshold (≥100 events) OR session-count threshold (≥5 sessions since last analysis) → emit notification \"background analysis ready; agent fires next turn\".\n299\t- First L-1 dispatch: classify event corpus into improvement categories (drift / retry / mistake-type / promotion-candidate). Use sonnet medium effort (capability-map row #5 grounded).\n300\t- Output: `agent-workspace/memory/learning-data/index/categories-<TS>.md` (deterministic-first index; LLM-classification appended).\n301\t\n302\t**Files affected**:\n303\t- NEW `scripts/hooks/learning-queue-sweeper.sh`\n304\t- NEW `scripts/hooks/learning-index-rebuild.sh`\n305\t- MODIFIED `.claude/settings.json` (hooks block: SessionStart + Stop additions)\n306\t- NEW `agent-workspace/memory/learning-data/index/categories-<TS>.md` (first analysis output)\n307\t\n308\t**Success**:\n309\t- Sweeper rotates ≥1 sample event end-to-end on dry-run\n310\t- Index rebuild produces queryable SQLite FTS5 db with ≥10 sample events indexed\n311\t- First L-1 classification run categorizes ≥10 sample events; output structured per template\n312\t- Background-dispatch trigger fires correctly on dry-run with mock 100-event state\n313\t\n314\t### Sub-track 5.5d.3 — Karpathy Outer Loop + Opensource Agent-Pick-1 Dogfood (~150K, 1 session = S12)\n315\t\n316\t**Goals**:\n317\t- Author `.claude/agents/research-scanner.md` (or reuse existing if shipped earlier in 5.5b/c): subagent that reads README + recent commits of opensource repos and reports fit-for-stockforge with provenance.\n318\t- Agent-pick-1 dogfood: pick the single most-aligned opensource self-learning/Karpathy/autoresearch repo (candidates: DSPy, LangSmith, llm.c-style autoresearch, openai/swarm, or other emerging). Selection criterion: maximally aligned with stockforge's \"harness self-learning from sessions/agent-notes/mistake-log\" use case.\n319\t- Dogfood the picked tool: integrate enough to produce 1 measurable insight (e.g., \"X agent-notes cluster pattern was missed by promote-rule but flagged by tool Y\"); document insight in `agent-workspace/memory/learning-data/dogfood/<tool>.md`.\n320\t- L-3 Karpathy outer loop framing: given dogfood data + 5.5d.2 first-analysis output, frame next experiment (deepen/broaden/abandon? what measurement?). They are different disciplines with different data shapes,\ndifferent cost profiles, and different boundaries. Conflating them in shared storage causes runtime\nto load write-only data accidentally (size + irrelevance penalty).\n\nUser-provided framing: **\"harness engineering = backend engineering / data ETL\"**. {"parentUuid":"f3c429c0-480c-4349-8458-4b0e61279aea","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"fc11436c-fada-41bc-8ae7-32ac3f984211","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01YVTsdYfxLj1CitRM66tU8B","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t---\n2\tschema_version: 1\n3\tcreated_at: 2026-04-29\n4\tupdated_at: 2026-04-29\n5\tdescription: |\n6\t  Living document tracking observed LLM capabilities and limits across model × effort × task_class.\n7\t  Read by `decompose-work` skill at decomposition time. Updated via `promote-rule` skill clusters.\n8\t  Source-grounded — every cell cites a concrete observation (agent-notes entry, charter invariant,\n9\t  drift-log finding, or post-mortem).\n10\tsource_decision: agent-workspace/memory/decisions/003-up06-track-5.5-sync-layer-selfcap.md\n11\tsource_intent: human-workspace/user_prompt/20260429_06.txt §3\n12\trelated_skills:\n13\t  - .claude/skills/decompose-work/SKILL.md  (consumer)\n14\t  - .claude/skills/promote-rule/SKILL.md     (writer)\n15\t  - .claude/skills/try-n-approaches/SKILL.md  (S9 — consumer)\n16\t---\n17\t\n18\t# Capability Map\n19\t\n20\t> Sparse cells. Append-only by default; cells may transition state with annotation.\n21\t> Per UP-06 §3: \"skill hoặc agent với khả năng chuyên biệt giúp 'bóc tách' deterministic vs llm probability\".\n22\t> This map IS the grounding for that decomposition.\n23\t\n24\t## Dimensions\n25\t\n26\t```yaml\n27\tmodel:      [opus-4.7, sonnet-4.6, haiku-4.5, any]   # \"any\" = model-agnostic (charter / architectural)\n28\teffort:     [low, medium, high, xhigh, max, any]\n29\ttask_class: <see below; appended as new task types observed>\n30\t```\n31\t\n32\t## Task Classes (current vocabulary)\n33\t\n34\t- **math** — numerical computation\n35\t- **synthesis** — multi-source narrative summary\n36\t- **design** — architecture / interface choice\n37\t- **naming** — short identifier creation (variables, files, clusters)\n38\t- **extraction** — claim extraction from unstructured text\n39\t- **classification** — fuzzy categorization (sentiment, drift severity, intent)\n40\t- **calibration** — confidence claim with hit-rate trace\n41\t- **adversarial** — bear/critic/devil's-advocate perspective\n42\t- **verification** — checking measurable properties against claims\n43\t- **qa-composition** — Q&A bundle authoring (incl. charter-tier split)\n44\t- **qa-input-channel** — surfacing questions through right surface (ask vs file)\n45\t- **intent-classification** — routing user prompts to actions\n46\t- **session-bootstrap** — SessionStart hook + first-prompt processing\n47\t- **bash-scripting** — shell scripts (hooks, automation)\n48\t- **adr-authoring** — decision records with provenance + delta summary\n49\t- **thesis-output** — stockforge thesis with bull/bear/calibration\n50\t- **data-reconciliation** — multi-source numeric divergence\n51\t\n52\t---\n53\t\n54\t## Strengths Observed\n55\t\n56\t| # | model | effort | task_class | observation | source |\n57\t|---|---|---|---|---|---|\n58\t| 1 | opus-4.7 | high | design | Multi-mechanism ensemble design (4-mechanism sync infra in 1 plan) lands well. Trade-off matrices natural. | agent-notes 2026-04-29 D-003 R1+R2 + sessions/S6+S7 |\n59\t| 2 | opus-4.7 | high | adversarial | Bear-case generation per thesis is reliable when prompted explicitly with `≥3 distinct points`. | agent-notes 2026-04-23 § Bear Case Required |\n60\t| 3 | opus-4.7 | medium | adr-authoring | 12-field schema ADR with provenance citations consistently produced when template provided. | sessions/S2..S7 D-001..D-004 |\n61\t| 4 | sonnet-4.6 | medium | extraction | Vietnamese KOL transcript claim extraction with structured JSON output is reliable when prompt enforces source_url + extracted_at fields. | evidence-extraction skill (planned Phase 2) + charter Tier 3 |\n62\t| 5 | sonnet-4.6 | low | intent-classification | Trivial-prompt detection (\"ok continue\", \"yes proceed\") cheap + accurate via lite-detect patterns. | agent-notes 2026-04-29 § ok continue idiom + lite-detect-patterns.md |\n63\t| 6 | haiku-4.5 | low | naming | Short identifier naming (cluster titles, variable names) acceptable when context narrowly scoped. | inferred — used for promote-rule cluster naming step |\n64\t| 7 | any | any | qa-composition | Bundle skeleton with `defer_cycle: 0` field at creation prevents stale-bundle drift. | agent-notes 2026-04-29 § Q&A Bundle Skeleton |\n65\t\n66\t---\n67\t\n68\t## Limits Observed\n69\t\n70\t| # | model | effort | task_class | observation | source |\n71\t|---|---|---|---|---|---|\n72\t| L-1 | any | any | math | NO-LLM-math invariant absolute (I-S1). LLM never outputs numbers it computed. | charter principle 9 + agent-notes 2026-04-23 § LLM Never Outputs Numbers |\n73\t| L-2 | any | any | calibration | \"X% confidence\" without n_samples + lookback = drift. Confidence MUST trace to historical hit rate. | charter principle 8 + agent-notes 2026-04-23 § Confidence Requires Calibration Data |\n74\t| L-3 | opus-4.7 | high | verification | False self-attestation on measurable properties (LOC, byte count, presence). Must use `wc -l`, `Glob`, `Grep` to verify; never estimate from memory. | agent-notes 2026-04-29 § AP-S2-3 + S2 drift audit |\n75\t| L-4 | opus-4.7 | high | adversarial | Same-agent self-review = echo chamber (AP-1). Always dispatch fresh-context Verifier. | patterns-discovered AP-1 + sandwich pattern doctrine |\n76\t| L-5 | any | any | qa-input-channel | File-only Q&A with default-after-N-hours = silent absorption. AskUserQuestion is the PRIMARY channel; file = audit only. | UP-04 + UP-06 + agent-notes 2026-04-29 § NO Silent File-Defaults |\n77\t| L-6 | any | any | qa-composition | Charter-tier questions in mixed-tier bundles get absorbed via \"ok continue\" → drift. Charter-tier MUST split into dedicated bundle. | agent-notes 2026-04-29 § Charter-Tier Items + post-audit G1 |\n78\t| L-7 | any | any | session-bootstrap | Stale-prompt references (UP-N closed work) without deterministic detection block autonomous flow. Hook-based detection is the only viable Guardian. | agent-notes 2026-04-29 § Stale-Prompt Reference Check + mistake-log M-S7-1 |\n79\t| L-8 | opus-4.7 | high | adr-authoring | REV-N with ≥10 amendments OR ≥25% budget delta needs explicit \"Delta Summary\" headline; otherwise user absorbs without seeing magnitude. | agent-notes 2026-04-29 § Pre-Amendment Delta Summary |\n80\t| L-9 | any | any | bash-scripting | `grep -c \\|\\| echo 0` pattern double-counts zero on no-match. Use `\\|\\| true` + regex validate instead. | agent-notes 2026-04-29 S3 § grep -c |\n81\t| L-10 | any | any | data-reconciliation | Multi-source numeric divergence (vnstock vs FiinPro) silently averaged = drift. Output divergence explicitly with provenance. | agent-notes 2026-04-23 § Source Provider Disagreement |\n82\t| L-11 | any | any | thesis-output | Single-perspective thesis = anti-pattern. Bear case ≥3 distinct points mandatory; refuse render without it. | charter principle 3 + agent-notes 2026-04-23 § Bear Case Required |\n83\t| L-12 | any | any | session-bootstrap | Continue-injector unconditional in SUPERVISED = race condition with user typing. MUST gate by autonomous_mode flag. | agent-notes 2026-04-29 § Continue-Injector Gated + mistake-log M-S7-1 L2 |\n84\t\n85\t---\n86\t\n87\t## Heuristics for Decompose-Work (consumer)\n88\t\n89\tWhen `decompose-work` skill classifies a sub-task:\n90\t\n91\t1. **Cite this map**: which strength row supports \"do via LLM\"? Which limit row forces \"do deterministic\"?\n92\t2. **Default to deterministic on conflict**: if a row is in *both* strengths AND limits → safer to assume LIMIT (under-promise).\n93\t3. **Map gap → propose entry**: if task_class is novel (not in vocabulary above), add tentative entry post-execution + flag `provisional: true`.\n94\t4. **Effort-tier matters**: a task_class strength at `effort=max` may be a limit at `effort=low`. Default to medium if unsure.\n95\t\n96\t## Promotion Path (writer = promote-rule skill)\n97\t\n98\tWhen `promote-rule` clusters agent-notes:\n99\t- Cluster naming → adds new task_class to vocabulary (if novel)\n100\t- Cluster of ≥3 limits with same task_class → likely capability-map limit row\n101\t- Cluster of ≥3 strengths with same model+effort+task_class → strength row\n102\t- Single-occurrence observations → DO NOT add to map (one-off, may not generalize)\n103\t\n104\t## Update Protocol\n105\t\n106\t- **From agent-notes via promote-rule** (S8+): `promote-rule` skill scans agent-notes; clusters of ≥3 → proposes capability-map entry. Agent reviews + writes.\n107\t- **From drift-log**: new limit observed → append to Limits Observed with source citation.\n108\t- **From thesis post-mortem** (Phase 1+): observed strength/limit per stock-domain task_class → append.\n109\t- **State transition**: if a previously-strength row becomes a limit (regression), mark old row `superseded-by: row-N` and add new row.\n110\t\n111\t## Anti-Patterns\n112\t\n113\t- Adding capability-map entries from speculation, not observation. Every cell cites a source.\n114\t- Single-occurrence \"X failed once\" → not a limit yet. Wait for ≥3 instances.\n115\t- Generalizing model-specific findings to `any` → keep model-tagged unless invariant.\n116\t- Skipping `effort` dimension → \"opus is good at design\" without effort tier loses signal.\n117\t- Treating limits as permanent → revisit on model upgrade (Opus 4.7 → 4.8 → 5.0 may invalidate).\n118\t\n119\t## Notes\n120\t\n121\t- Counts: 7 strengths + 12 limits = 19 total entries (≥10 S8 target ✓).\n122\t- Skill capability claim is a future state — try-n-approaches skill (S9) will explicitly track approach×model×task_class outcomes; that data flows here.\n123\t- For stockforge stock-domain task classes (thesis synthesis, KOL extraction, pump detection) — most cells assumed-aligned until Phase 1+ dogfood data accumulates.\n124\t"}]},"uuid":"51a23066-c494-4239-8890-453fdf681fc9","timestamp":"2026-04-29T13:07:29.962Z","toolUseResult":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\agent-workspace\\memory\\capability-map.md","content":"---\nschema_version: 1\ncreated_at: 2026-04-29\nupdated_at: 2026-04-29\ndescription: |\n  Living document tracking observed LLM capabilities and limits across model × effort × task_class.\n  Read by `decompose-work` skill at decomposition time.
