Authoring focused dogfood insight artifact at `learning-data/dogfood/dspy.md` (separate from the survey report)."}],"stop_reason":"tool_use","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":10420,"cache_read_input_tokens":128251,"output_tokens":4513,"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":10420,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0},"inference_geo":"","iterations":[{"input_tokens":1,"output_tokens":4513,"cache_read_input_tokens":128251,"cache_creation_input_tokens":10420,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":10420},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011CaY89YVWSoEVxM1inkSXP","type":"assistant","uuid":"63a2cc94-c923-40ab-b94e-fa6787107641","timestamp":"2026-04-29T13:14:51.540Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"45ff2d8f-d633-45f8-8a6e-c5fb5ce08842","version":"2.1.122","gitBranch":"main"}
{"parentUuid":"63a2cc94-c923-40ab-b94e-fa6787107641","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-7","id":"msg_012RXHrc8N1NcmNYZuxGsqm5","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_018gx8aMYsULJz3D414tdTtP","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\agent-workspace\\learning-data\\dogfood\\dspy.md","content":"---\nartifact_id: dogfood-dspy-20260429\ncreated_at: 2026-04-29\nsession: S12 (Track 5.5d.3)\ntool_studied: stanfordnlp/dspy\ntool_sha: db83e5ad7154ee2e31f2cdd4f13351ded47a23d3\ntool_as_of: 2026-04-29\nlicense: MIT\nupstream_url: https://github.com/stanfordnlp/dspy\nresearch_report_ref: agent-workspace/learning-data/dogfood/agent-pick-1-research-report-20260429T131201Z.md\n---\n\n# Dogfood Insight — DSPy → StockForge Self-Learning Loop\n\n> Pattern-level extraction (NOT integration). Phase-0 harness is bash + NDJSON; DSPy is Python module framework. The dogfood signal is structural mapping, not execution. Per D-005 § 5.5d.3 + research-scanner agent-pick-1 pick (sha `db83e5ad`, as-of 2026-04-29).\n\n## Setup (what was actually done)\n\n- Survey: 6 candidates scanned by `research-scanner` subagent (S12 dispatch, sonnet, ~50K tokens, 213s wall-clock). DSPy picked over openevals (runner-up); 4 disqualifications. See `agent-pick-1-research-report-20260429T131201Z.md` for full provenance.\n- Source-read (this artifact): README header (`stanfordnlp/dspy@db83e5ad/README.md`) + paper title (`DSPy: Compiling Declarative Language Model Calls into Self-Improving Pipelines`, 2023-10) + scoring matrix in research report. Did NOT clone repo; did NOT pip install; did NOT execute optimizer.\n\n## The one insight (≥1 measurable)\n\n**StockForge's self-learning loop is missing the deterministic metric function that DSPy treats as the load-bearing bridge between signal and improvement.** S11's L-1 classifier (sonnet/medium) produced bucket distributions (0/0/0/49/11) — but emitted **no metric**. Without a metric, no version of the pipeline can be said to be \"better\" than another. The compile-loop framing collapses without it.\n\nDSPy's pattern (verbatim from research-report § 1):\n> \"DSPy optimizer requires a `metric` function that returns a score from deterministic code — the LLM does not score itself.\"\n\nMapped onto StockForge's loop:\n\n| DSPy concept | StockForge counterpart | Status today |\n|---|---|---|\n| `dspy.Module` (LLM-call wrapper) | Hook script + LLM dispatch + telemetry emit | EXISTS (component-telemetry.sh + Agent dispatches) |\n| Labeled examples | Sessions × (action taken, retrospective verdict) | PARTIAL (sessions logs exist; verdicts tagged informally in agent-notes) |\n| **Metric function (deterministic)** | **MISSING — S11 L-1 has buckets but no scalar/categorical \"this run was better than last\"** | **GAP** |\n| Optimizer (BootstrapFewShot, MIPROv2, COPRO) | promote-rule skill + future try-n-approaches | PARTIAL (promote-rule ships; try-n-approaches S13) |\n| Compiled improved pipeline | Updated capability-map + new hook/skill/charter rule | PARTIAL (cap-map updates manual today) |\n\n**The measurable consequence**: until a metric exists, the Karpathy outer-loop framing artifact (also S12 deliverable) cannot answer \"was this experiment better than the prior baseline?\" — it can only describe what was tried, not what was earned.\n\n### Concrete metric candidates for StockForge (≥1 must be picked at S13)\n\nPer DSPy's discipline that the metric function is **deterministic Python** (no LLM math), candidate metrics for StockForge's loop:\n\n1. **failure_mode population rate** (deterministic): `count(events where failure_mode != null) / count(events total)`. Today: 0/60 = 0% (per S11 L-1 categories file). After fix to `correlate_failure_mode()` wire-in: target ≥ 5% on next 60-event corpus. Falsifiable, code-computed, no LLM judgment.\n\n2. **drift signal precision** (deterministic, requires labels): for each D1-D9 fire, was a corrective action taken in the next 3 sessions? `count(D-fire with subsequent fix) / count(D-fire total)`. Requires lightweight labeling protocol (1 sentence per D-fire in session log).\n\n3. **Promotion-candidate hit rate** (deterministic, longitudinal): of the 5 components flagged promotion-candidate at S11 (Read, Bash, TaskUpdate, Edit, Write), how many become formally promoted to skill/hook within 3 sessions? `count(promoted) / count(flagged)`. Today: 0/5 = 0% (none promoted yet). Target after S15 (Track 7): ≥ 1.\n\n4. **Re-classification stability** (deterministic, regression metric): re-run L-1 on the same 60-event corpus with the next-month classifier. Compare bucket distribution. Drift > 10% across runs = classifier regression signal.\n\nThese are the kind of metrics DSPy's pattern enforces: the metric function returns a number from code, the LLM never grades itself.\n\n## What was rejected from DSPy\n\nPer research report adversarial bear point #4 (\"LLM-as-judge risk in optimizer\"): **adopt DSPy's metric-function pattern only; reject MIPRO-style LLM-instruction-generation optimizers for now.** MIPRO uses an LLM to propose candidate instructions inside the optimization loop — that re-introduces LLM math into a path the charter (P9) explicitly forbids. StockForge's first dogfood adoption is structural (the pattern of a deterministic bridge between signal and improvement), not infrastructural (running DSPy modules in the harness).\n\n## Bear-case awareness for downstream sessions\n\nFrom research report adversarial section, ≥3 risks transfer to dogfood adoption:\n\n- **Frontier-model substitution (6-12 months)**: as Claude 5/Sonnet 5 generalize from minimal examples natively, prompt-optimization patterns may shrink in marginal value. Re-evaluate metric-function adoption value at next major model bump. Not blocking now.\n- **Abstraction mismatch (current)**: bash + NDJSON ≠ Python `dspy.Module`. Translation tax is real. Mitigation here: extract pattern (deterministic metric function), not infrastructure (DSPy package itself).\n- **Stanford research governance**: DSPy is Stanford Future Data Systems lab; PhD-driven, not production-feedback-driven. Last 5 commits are docs/CI (per research report). If lab graduates → fork/maintenance risk. Mitigation: stockforge depends on the *idea* (metric function), which is portable across any future framework.\n\n## Decision (S12 closes with this)\n\n**Adopt**: DSPy's deterministic-metric-function pattern as the missing piece between L-1 classification (S11 ship) and try-n-approaches outer loop (S13 plan).\n\n**Defer**: Direct DSPy infrastructure adoption. No `pip install dspy`. No `dspy.Module` wrappers. No MIPRO. Phase 0 harness stays bash + NDJSON.\n\n**Surface to capability-map**: NEW task_class candidate `tool-survey` (proven by this S12 first dogfood — research-scanner agent + ≤5-page report + adversarial bear case + provenance log = repeatable pattern).\n\n**Surface to agent-notes**: NEW rule on metric-function-required-before-loop-claims (see § Promotion path below).\n\n## Promotion path (closes loop per D-005 § 5.5d.3 success criterion #4)\n\nThis dogfood produces ≥1 promotable rule:\n\n> **Rule candidate**: Any \"self-learning\" or \"Karpathy outer-loop\" claim made by stockforge (now or future) MUST be backed by a deterministic metric function (Python or bash, no LLM judgment) before the claim counts as substantiated. If no metric → describe the experiment, do NOT claim improvement.\n\nPromotion target priority (per Q-E3 queued-grill answer template hook>skill>charter, cheapest first):\n- **Hook**: `scripts/hooks/learning-loop-metric-check.sh` — scans `learning-data/loop/*-experiment-frame.md` for missing `metric_function:` frontmatter field; soft-warn at Stop hook. Cheapest enforcement.\n- **Skill**: extend `try-n-approaches` (S13) to require metric-function arg before allowing deepen/broaden/abandon framing.\n- **Charter**: amend `agent-workspace/constitution/financial-data-protocol.md` § Calibration with explicit \"no metric → no improvement claim\" line. Most expensive; reserve for if hook+skill insufficient.\n\nFinal promotion target chosen by S13 promote-rule pass once corpus has ≥3 supporting instances. Today: 1 instance (this dogfood); below promotion threshold; queue + observe.\n\n## Provenance\n\n| Source | URL / Path | SHA / TS | as-of |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| DSPy README header | https://github.com/stanfordnlp/dspy/blob/main/README.md | `db83e5ad` | 2026-04-29 |\n| DSPy paper title | DSPy README references paper \"DSPy: Compiling Declarative Language Model Calls into Self-Improving Pipelines\" (2023-10) | n/a | 2026-04-29 |\n| Research report (sibling artifact) | agent-workspace/learning-data/dogfood/agent-pick-1-research-report-20260429T131201Z.md | n/a | 2026-04-29 |\n| S11 L-1 categories (input data) | agent-workspace/learning-data/index/categories-20260429T123210Z.md | n/a | 2026-04-29 |\n| D-005 § 5.5d.3 (deliverable spec) | agent-workspace/memory/decisions/005-up08-track-5.5d-self-learning-pipeline.md | n/a | 2026-04-29 |\n\n## What this insight is NOT\n\n- NOT a recommendation to adopt DSPy as a runtime dependency.\n- NOT a measurement of stockforge's current loop quality (no metric exists yet — that IS the insight).\n- NOT a license assessment for production use (MIT acceptable for pattern extraction; for code copy-paste, license-header propagation required).\n- NOT a stock thesis or financial output. Pure harness-engineering artifact.\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":"tool_use","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":10420,"cache_read_input_tokens":128251,"output_tokens":4513,"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":10420,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0},"inference_geo":"","iterations":[{"input_tokens":1,"output_tokens":4513,"cache_read_input_tokens":128251,"cache_creation_input_tokens":10420,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":10420},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011CaY89YVWSoEVxM1inkSXP","type":"assistant","uuid":"6bc0700d-707f-45a0-bb83-9f77e8363e6d","timestamp":"2026-04-29T13:15:55.415Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"45ff2d8f-d633-45f8-8a6e-c5fb5ce08842","version":"2.1.122","gitBranch":"main"}

Compare bucket distribution. Drift > 10% across runs = classifier regression signal.\n\nThese are the kind of metrics DSPy's pattern enforces: the metric function returns a number from code, the LLM never grades itself.\n\n## What was rejected from DSPy\n\nPer research report adversarial bear point #4 (\"LLM-as-judge risk in optimizer\"): **adopt DSPy's metric-function pattern only; reject MIPRO-style LLM-instruction-generation optimizers for now.** MIPRO uses an LLM to propose candidate instructions inside the optimization loop — that re-introduces LLM math into a path the charter (P9) explicitly forbids. StockForge's first dogfood adoption is structural (the pattern of a deterministic bridge between signal and improvement), not infrastructural (running DSPy modules in the harness).\n\n## Bear-case awareness for downstream sessions\n\nFrom research report adversarial section, ≥3 risks transfer to dogfood adoption:\n\n- **Frontier-model substitution (6-12 months)**: as Claude 5/Sonnet 5 generalize from minimal examples natively, prompt-optimization patterns may shrink in marginal value. Re-evaluate metric-function adoption value at next major model bump. Not blocking now.\n- **Abstraction mismatch (current)**: bash + NDJSON ≠ Python `dspy.Module`. Translation tax is real. Mitigation here: extract pattern (deterministic metric function), not infrastructure (DSPy package itself).\n- **Stanford research governance**: DSPy is Stanford Future Data Systems lab; PhD-driven, not production-feedback-driven. Last 5 commits are docs/CI (per research report). If lab graduates → fork/maintenance risk. Mitigation: stockforge depends on the *idea* (metric function), which is portable across any future framework.\n\n## Decision (S12 closes with this)\n\n**Adopt**: DSPy's deterministic-metric-function pattern as the missing piece between L-1 classification (S11 ship) and try-n-approaches outer loop (S13 plan).\n\n**Defer**: Direct DSPy infrastructure adoption. No `pip install dspy`. No `dspy.Module` wrappers. No MIPRO. Phase 0 harness stays bash + NDJSON.\n\n**Surface to capability-map**: NEW task_class candidate `tool-survey` (proven by this S12 first dogfood — research-scanner agent + ≤5-page report + adversarial bear case + provenance log = repeatable pattern).\n\n**Surface to agent-notes**: NEW rule on metric-function-required-before-loop-claims (see § Promotion path below).\n\n## Promotion path (closes loop per D-005 § 5.5d.3 success criterion #4)\n\nThis dogfood produces ≥1 promotable rule:\n\n> **Rule candidate**: Any \"self-learning\" or \"Karpathy outer-loop\" claim made by stockforge (now or future) MUST be backed by a deterministic metric function (Python or bash, no LLM judgment) before the claim counts as substantiated. If no metric → describe the experiment, do NOT claim improvement.\n\nPromotion target priority (per Q-E3 queued-grill answer template hook>skill>charter, cheapest first):\n- **Hook**: `scripts/hooks/learning-loop-metric-check.sh` — scans `learning-data/loop/*-experiment-frame.md` for missing `metric_function:` frontmatter field; soft-warn at Stop hook. Cheapest enforcement.\n- **Skill**: extend `try-n-approaches` (S13) to require metric-function arg before allowing deepen/broaden/abandon framing.\n- **Charter**: amend `agent-workspace/constitution/financial-data-protocol.md` § Calibration with explicit \"no metric → no improvement claim\" line. Most expensive; reserve for if hook+skill insufficient.\n\nFinal promotion target chosen by S13 promote-rule pass once corpus has ≥3 supporting instances. Today: 1 instance (this dogfood); below promotion threshold; queue + observe.\n\n## Provenance\n\n| Source | URL / Path | SHA / TS | as-of |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| DSPy README header | https://github.com/stanfordnlp/dspy/blob/main/README.md | `db83e5ad` | 2026-04-29 |\n| DSPy paper title | DSPy README references paper \"DSPy: Compiling Declarative Language Model Calls into Self-Improving Pipelines\" (2023-10) | n/a | 2026-04-29 |\n| Research report (sibling artifact) | agent-workspace/learning-data/dogfood/agent-pick-1-research-report-20260429T131201Z.md | n/a | 2026-04-29 |\n| S11 L-1 categories (input data) | agent-workspace/learning-data/index/categories-20260429T123210Z.md | n/a | 2026-04-29 |\n| D-005 § 5.5d.3 (deliverable spec) | agent-workspace/memory/decisions/005-up08-track-5.5d-self-learning-pipeline.md | n/a | 2026-04-29 |\n\n## What this insight is NOT\n\n- NOT a recommendation to adopt DSPy as a runtime dependency.\n- NOT a measurement of stockforge's current loop quality (no metric exists yet — that IS the insight).\n- NOT a license assessment for production use (MIT acceptable for pattern extraction; for code copy-paste, license-header propagation required).\n- NOT a stock thesis or financial output. {"parentUuid":"e13ed993-cdc4-4fde-82fc-436cf6301677","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-7","id":"msg_01L9nmBCnfzaNBWdbhtEg4Bn","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_014aJYCviViBdxQ31RabnFAX","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\agent-workspace\\learning-data\\loop\\20260429T131608Z-experiment-frame.md","content":"---\nexperiment_id: loop-frame-20260429T131608Z\ncreated_at: 2026-04-29T13:16:08Z\nsession: S12 (Track 5.5d.3)\nframing_decision: DEEPEN\ninputs:\n  - agent-workspace/learning-data/index/categories-20260429T123210Z.md  # S11 L-1 first classification (60 events; 0/0/0/49/11 distribution)\n  - agent-workspace/learning-data/dogfood/dspy.md                       # S12 DSPy pattern insight (metric-function gap)\n  - agent-workspace/learning-data/dogfood/agent-pick-1-research-report-20260429T131201Z.md  # S12 research-scanner survey\nas_of: 2026-04-29\nmetric_function: not-yet-authored  # this experiment IS the prerequisite to authoring one\n---\n\n# Karpathy Outer-Loop Frame — Experiment #1\n\n> First Karpathy autoresearch framing for stockforge harness self-learning. Per D-005 § 5.5d.3\n> Phase 7 + UP-08 verbatim \"kết hợp với idea của karpathy autoresearch\". Pattern from\n> `.claude/skills/decompose-work/SKILL.md` (try-n-approaches not yet shipped — S13).\n\n## Question\n\nGiven the corpus state at S12 close (60 events / all-ok / no failure_mode populated / 5 promotion-candidate components) and the DSPy dogfood insight (deterministic metric function is the missing bridge), what is the next experiment that maximizes information gain per token spent?\n\n## Prior state — what S11 + S12 produced\n\n**S11 (Track 5.5d.2)**: First L-1 classification dispatch. 60 events. Distribution `0/0/0/49/11` (drift / retry / mistake-type / promotion-candidate / uncategorized). Six adversarial caveats documented; two are load-bearing for this framing:\n\n- Caveat #1: drift bucket may be structurally undercount — `component-telemetry.sh` may not emit on denied tool calls.\n- Caveat #2: `failure_mode` field is null on 60/60 events — possibly uninstrumented, not falsified.\n\n**S12 (Track 5.5d.3)**: Agent-pick-1 dogfood. DSPy picked. Pattern insight: stockforge has a self-learning loop with no metric function — without a deterministic scalar/categorical comparing run N+1 to run N, no version of the pipeline can be claimed \"better than\" another.\n\n## Three options on the table\n\n### Option A — DEEPEN (instrument the missing rungs)\n\n**Action**: Wire `failure_mode` field population in `component-telemetry.sh` from `correlate_failure_mode()` already drafted in `.autonomous-stop-watchdog.log` reads. Then author **one** deterministic metric function (the `failure_mode population rate` from dogfood/dspy.md § metric candidates) and re-run L-1 on a fresh ≥60-event corpus to compare.\n\n**What it costs**: ~1 hook-edit session (~30-50K). One metric script (~30 LOC bash or python). One re-dispatch of L-1 classifier (~36K, observed cost from S11). Estimated total: 1 session ≤100K. Fits S13/S14 alongside try-n-approaches ship.\n\n**Why this option**: Two highest-confidence caveats from S11 (drift undercount + failure_mode null) both resolve via the same fix path. The DSPy dogfood ALSO points to the same gap (metric function). Three independent signals (S11 caveat #1, caveat #2, DSPy insight) converging on one fix = strong deepen prior.\n\n**What we measure** (explicit, falsifiable):\n- Metric: `failure_mode_populated = count(events where failure_mode != null) / count(events total)`\n- Baseline (S11): 0/60 = 0.0%\n- Target (S12+1): ≥ 5/60 = 8.3% on next 60-event corpus\n- Falsification: if after instrumentation, the rate is still 0% AND no failures occurred = harness too stable to instrument; loop signal exhausted; PIVOT to Option B (broaden corpus to find rare events). If rate ≥ 8.3% = instrumentation works; advance to second metric (drift signal precision).\n\n### Option B — BROADEN (grow the corpus)\n\n**Action**: Increase corpus from 60 events to 200+ before next L-1 dispatch. Currently events accumulate naturally per session (~30-60 events per major session per S11 observation). Wait 3-4 sessions and re-run L-1.\n\n**What it costs**: 0 token cost in new sessions (events emit free); 1 re-dispatch (~36K).\n\n**Why this option might be tempting**: bigger corpus = more statistical power for rare-event detection (the <5% pattern blind-spot from S11 caveat #5).\n\n**Why this option is wrong now**: at 0% failure_mode population, broadening to 200 events still yields 0/200 — adding null data doesn't improve signal. Broaden is the right move AFTER instrumentation fix; before fix, it just delays the same problem.\n\n### Option C — ABANDON (delete the zero-signal buckets)\n\n**Action**: Delete drift / retry / mistake-type categories from L-1 spec; keep only promotion-candidate + uncategorized. Acknowledge harness is too clean to have negative signal worth tracking at Phase 0 scale.\n\n**Why this option might be tempting**: simpler classifier; no instrumentation work needed.\n\n**Why this option is wrong**: it confuses \"field uninstrumented\" with \"signal absent\". S11 caveat #2 explicitly noted: \"`mistake-type` and `drift` buckets are not falsified — they are simply uninstrumented.\" Abandoning a bucket because the field hasn't been wired = closing the loop on negative information that may exist. Charter principle 3 (adversarial by design) prefers asking \"is the absence real\" over \"assume absence is real\".\n\n## Picked direction — DEEPEN (Option A)\n\nRationale: convergence of 3 independent signals on the same fix; Option B requires Option A first; Option C confuses uninstrumented with absent (charter P3 violation).\n\nCheap-first ordering doctrine satisfied: A is the smallest unit of work that resolves the most caveats per token spent.\n\n## Measurement plan (explicit; this is the metric function S13 will codify)\n\n```\nmetric: failure_mode_populated_rate\nformula: count(events where failure_mode IS NOT null) / count(events total)\nimplementation: bash + python3 oneliner in scripts/hooks/learning-index-rebuild.sh\n                (extend existing hook; do NOT add new infra)\nbaseline:    0.0% (S11 corpus, n=60, observed 2026-04-29)\ntarget:      >= 8.3% (S12+1 corpus, n>=60, observed at first L-1 re-dispatch)\nfalsification: if rate < 8.3% AND no failures occurred in the corpus window\n               (deterministically: count(.autonomous-stop-watchdog.log entries) > 0\n                vs count(events with failure_mode != null) — if log shows failures\n                but events show null, instrumentation fix was incomplete)\ncalibration:   record n_samples + lookback_window in result; per charter P8\n```\n\nThis metric is **deterministic** (counts, no LLM judgment), **falsifiable** (specific threshold), **calibration-aware** (records sample size + lookback per L-2), and **cheap** (extends an existing hook; no new dependency). Matches DSPy's metric-function pattern as adapted in dogfood/dspy.md.\n\n## Risks (≥3 distinct, per charter P3 adversarial)\n\n1. **Failure mode classifier drift**: if `correlate_failure_mode()` is buggy (e.g., over-classifies stop-watchdog entries as failures), the rate will jump artificially. Mitigation: smoke-test the function with `LEARNING_INDEX_FORCE=1` on known events before declaring the metric trusted.\n\n2. **Corpus window mismatch**: 60 next events may span a heavily-IMPL session (high tool counts, mostly ok) or a heavily-PLAN session (lower tool counts, more decision points). Distribution variance vs prior corpus could swamp the instrumentation signal. Mitigation: log session_type per event in next telemetry pass; segment corpus by type at L-1 time.\n\n3. **Premature optimization**: focusing on metric authoring before second L-1 baseline run could lead to over-fitting to S11's specific blind spots. Mitigation: don't promote the metric function to charter (Track 7) until ≥3 corpus runs confirm the rate trends in interpretable directions.\n\n4. **Frontier-model substitution risk on classifier**: by S13, sonnet-4.6 may be replaced by sonnet-5; classifier output distribution may shift even on the same corpus. Mitigation: lock classifier model + effort in `categories-<TS>.md` frontmatter (S11 already does this — `classifier_model: sonnet`, `classifier_effort: medium`). Compare runs at same model version; flag cross-version comparisons explicitly.\n\n## What the next experiment is NOT\n\n- NOT a thesis on stocks. Pure harness-engineering loop.\n- NOT a request to ship a new skill or hook this session — the framing IS the artifact; the wiring + metric function is S13's work (per current-execution.md sequence).\n- NOT a claim that DSPy itself will be adopted. Only its metric-function pattern is borrowed.\n- NOT optimization of any frontend / runtime path. Background analysis loop only.\n\n## Provenance\n\n| Source | Path | as-of |\n|---|---|---|\n| S11 L-1 first classification | agent-workspace/learning-data/index/categories-20260429T123210Z.md | 2026-04-29 |\n| S11 session log (caveats #1+#2 origin) | agent-workspace/memory/sessions/2026-04-29-session-11.md § L-1 dispatch | 2026-04-29 |\n| S12 DSPy dogfood insight | agent-workspace/learning-data/dogfood/dspy.md | 2026-04-29 |\n| S12 research-scanner report | agent-workspace/learning-data/dogfood/agent-pick-1-research-report-20260429T131201Z.md | 2026-04-29 |\n| D-005 § 5.5d.3 | agent-workspace/memory/decisions/005-up08-track-5.5d-self-learning-pipeline.md | 2026-04-29 |\n| Charter principles 1, 3, 8, 9 | PROJECT_CHARTER.md | 2026-04-29 |\n| Capability-map L-1 (no LLM math) + L-2 (calibration metadata) | agent-workspace/memory/capability-map.md | 2026-04-29 |\n\n## Outcome (filled at next loop iteration)\n\n- next_dispatch_target: S13 (Track 5.5c.3+4+5 try-n-approaches + OTEL + JSONL extension; metric-function authoring fits naturally here)\n- expected_corpus_at_next_l1: 100-150 events (cumulative through S13 close)\n- decision_at_outcome_review: deepen confirmed | broaden | abandon — to be filled when next L-1 runs\n\n---\n\n*Generated S12 closing. Author: claude-opus-4-7. Per UP-08 directive 'thêm sớm' (early instrumentation enables later measurement).*\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":"tool_use","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":959,"cache_read_input_tokens":143238,"output_tokens":4378,"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":959,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0},"inference_geo":"","iterations":[{"input_tokens":1,"output_tokens":4378,"cache_read_input_tokens":143238,"cache_creation_input_tokens":959,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":959},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011CaY8Gdw8ZUAGyJwrdnVfY","type":"assistant","uuid":"27fe9185-9efd-49fc-9def-9f83fdb7bc98","timestamp":"2026-04-29T13:17:24.186Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"45ff2d8f-d633-45f8-8a6e-c5fb5ce08842","version":"2.1.122","gitBranch":"main"}

Promote to formal rule in `agent-workspace/constitution/decision-discipline.md` § \"When spec inconsistency surfaces at IMPL time\" at Track 7 (S15).","new_string":"**Auto-detect**: partial — drift-detector D4 already scans for spec ↔ code dangling refs. Extension: when same decision file references two paths for the \"same artifact concept\" within different sections, flag as INTERNAL-INCONSISTENCY. Promote to formal rule in `agent-workspace/constitution/decision-discipline.md` § \"When spec inconsistency surfaces at IMPL time\" at Track 7 (S15).\n\n### 2026-04-29 (S12 / Track 5.5d.3 dogfood): Self-Learning / Outer-Loop Claims Require Deterministic Metric Function\n**Context**: S12 dogfooded `stanfordnlp/dspy@db83e5ad` (research-scanner dispatch result, agent-pick-1 from D-005 § 5.5d.3). Pattern insight from DSPy: every self-improving pipeline's optimizer requires a `metric` function returning a score from deterministic code — the LLM does not score itself. Mapping that onto stockforge's harness surfaced a gap: S11's L-1 classification produced bucket distributions (`0/0/0/49/11`) but emitted **no metric** comparing run N+1 to run N. Without a metric, no version of the loop can be substantiated as \"improved\" over baseline. The Karpathy outer-loop framing artifact (S12 D5) cannot answer \"was the experiment better than the prior baseline?\" without one.\n**Rule**: Any \"self-learning\", \"Karpathy outer-loop\", or \"harness-improvement\" claim authored in stockforge (now or future) MUST cite a deterministic metric function (Python or bash, no LLM judgment) with: (a) explicit formula, (b) baseline value with `n_samples` + `as_of`, (c) target threshold, (d) falsification condition. If no metric exists → describe the experiment (\"we tried X\"), do NOT claim improvement (\"X improved Y\"). Per charter P8 calibration-over-confidence + P9 no-LLM-math.\n**Anti-example**: A Karpathy framing artifact that says \"we deepened classification quality by adding fail-mode wire-in\" without a metric to prove the deepening — the claim collapses to vibes; future agent reading it cannot replicate the verdict.\n**Correct example**: `agent-workspace/learning-data/loop/20260429T131608Z-experiment-frame.md` § Measurement plan — declares `failure_mode_populated_rate = count(events.failure_mode!=null) / count(events)`; baseline 0.0% (n=60, S11); target ≥8.3%; falsification = \"rate stays 0% AND log shows failures occurred\". Deterministic, falsifiable, calibrated.\n**Severity**: high (without this, every self-learning iteration becomes a vibes-based feedback loop — exact failure mode charter principle 8 was written to prevent).\n**Auto-detect**: yes — Track 5.5d.2 `learning-index-rebuild.sh` can be extended (S13) to scan `learning-data/loop/*-experiment-frame.md` for missing `metric_function:` frontmatter field; soft-warn at Stop hook. Promotion target priority hook>skill>charter (cheapest first) per D-005 § 5.5d.3 promotion path.\n\n### 2026-04-29 (S12 / Track 5.5d.3 dogfood): Research-Scanner Dispatch Discipline — Provenance + Adversarial + License Required\n**Context**: S12 first dispatch of `.claude/agents/research-scanner.md` produced a 5-page survey of 6 candidate repos in ~213s wall-clock + 53K tokens (sonnet, run_in_background=true). Output structured per agent-file template: per-candidate findings with verbatim README quotes + commit SHAs + as-of dates; scoring matrix (5×6, scores cite source); adversarial bear case (4 distinct points); provenance log (18 URL+SHA+as-of rows). Critically, the adversarial scan caught `openai/swarm` is dead since 2025-03 (>400 days, README explicitly redirects to OpenAI Agents SDK) — a candidate that \"minimal orchestration\" framing might have led an unreviewed pick to choose. Scan was the only thing standing between \"shallow seed-list pick\" and \"informed adversarial selection\".\n**Rule**: Every research-scanner dispatch MUST produce: (a) provenance log with repo URL + commit SHA-7 + as-of date for every cited fact; (b) license field per candidate (skip-fast disqualifies AGPL/proprietary if use case is permissive-only); (c) scoring on stockforge-specific axes with each score citing a verbatim README/commit quote (no \"feel\" scoring); (d) ≥3-distinct-point adversarial bear case for the picked candidate (maintenance staleness / license / integration cost / frontier-model substitution); (e) ≥1 disqualifier surfaced for at least one rejected candidate (signal: scan was adversarial, not just confirming). If <3 bear points emerge → assume scan was shallow; lengthen Phase 3-4 fact-gathering, do not ship the report.\n**Anti-example**: A 1-page summary \"DSPy looks great, license MIT, recent commits\" with no SHAs, no quotes, no adversarial section — invoker cannot audit the pick; six months later when DSPy lab graduates and the repo stalls, no one remembers why it was picked over runner-up.\n**Correct example**: `agent-workspace/learning-data/dogfood/agent-pick-1-research-report-20260429T131201Z.md` — 18-row provenance log, scores cite README lines, 4 bear points (frontier substitution / abstraction mismatch / Stanford research governance / LLM-as-judge in MIPRO), `openai/swarm` disqualified with explicit \">400 days since substantive commit + README explicitly redirects\" reason.\n**Severity**: high (a research scan that's not adversarial is an echo chamber for the seed list; AP-1 same-agent-self-review applies — pick-without-bear-case is structurally unfalsifiable).\n**Auto-detect**: yes — Stop hook can grep new `learning-data/dogfood/research-*.md` for required frontmatter (`picked:` + `as_of:`) + section headers (`## Adversarial Bear Case` + `## Provenance Log`); soft-warn if missing. Cheap enforcement; promotion target = Track 5.5d hook extension."},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":"tool_use","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1489,"cache_read_input_tokens":151137,"output_tokens":2797,"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":1489,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0},"inference_geo":"","iterations":[{"input_tokens":1,"output_tokens":2797,"cache_read_input_tokens":151137,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1489,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":1489},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011CaY8RL4M2nKYng9tXJftH","type":"assistant","uuid":"3750914d-9796-419f-bda2-ed2a064696e7","timestamp":"2026-04-29T13:19:01.217Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"45ff2d8f-d633-45f8-8a6e-c5fb5ce08842","version":"2.1.122","gitBranch":"main"}

Promote to formal rule in `agent-workspace/constitution/decision-discipline.md` § \"When spec inconsistency surfaces at IMPL time\" at Track 7 (S15).","newString":"**Auto-detect**: partial — drift-detector D4 already scans for spec ↔ code dangling refs. Extension: when same decision file references two paths for the \"same artifact concept\" within different sections, flag as INTERNAL-INCONSISTENCY. Promote to formal rule in `agent-workspace/constitution/decision-discipline.md` § \"When spec inconsistency surfaces at IMPL time\" at Track 7 (S15).\n\n### 2026-04-29 (S12 / Track 5.5d.3 dogfood): Self-Learning / Outer-Loop Claims Require Deterministic Metric Function\n**Context**: S12 dogfooded `stanfordnlp/dspy@db83e5ad` (research-scanner dispatch result, agent-pick-1 from D-005 § 5.5d.3). Pattern insight from DSPy: every self-improving pipeline's optimizer requires a `metric` function returning a score from deterministic code — the LLM does not score itself. Mapping that onto stockforge's harness surfaced a gap: S11's L-1 classification produced bucket distributions (`0/0/0/49/11`) but emitted **no metric** comparing run N+1 to run N. Without a metric, no version of the loop can be substantiated as \"improved\" over baseline. The Karpathy outer-loop framing artifact (S12 D5) cannot answer \"was the experiment better than the prior baseline?\" without one.\n**Rule**: Any \"self-learning\", \"Karpathy outer-loop\", or \"harness-improvement\" claim authored in stockforge (now or future) MUST cite a deterministic metric function (Python or bash, no LLM judgment) with: (a) explicit formula, (b) baseline value with `n_samples` + `as_of`, (c) target threshold, (d) falsification condition. If no metric exists → describe the experiment (\"we tried X\"), do NOT claim improvement (\"X improved Y\"). Per charter P8 calibration-over-confidence + P9 no-LLM-math.\n**Anti-example**: A Karpathy framing artifact that says \"we deepened classification quality by adding fail-mode wire-in\" without a metric to prove the deepening — the claim collapses to vibes; future agent reading it cannot replicate the verdict.\n**Correct example**: `agent-workspace/learning-data/loop/20260429T131608Z-experiment-frame.md` § Measurement plan — declares `failure_mode_populated_rate = count(events.failure_mode!=null) / count(events)`; baseline 0.0% (n=60, S11); target ≥8.3%; falsification = \"rate stays 0% AND log shows failures occurred\". Deterministic, falsifiable, calibrated.\n**Severity**: high (without this, every self-learning iteration becomes a vibes-based feedback loop — exact failure mode charter principle 8 was written to prevent).\n**Auto-detect**: yes — Track 5.5d.2 `learning-index-rebuild.sh` can be extended (S13) to scan `learning-data/loop/*-experiment-frame.md` for missing `metric_function:` frontmatter field; soft-warn at Stop hook. Promotion target priority hook>skill>charter (cheapest first) per D-005 § 5.5d.3 promotion path.\n\n### 2026-04-29 (S12 / Track 5.5d.3 dogfood): Research-Scanner Dispatch Discipline — Provenance + Adversarial + License Required\n**Context**: S12 first dispatch of `.claude/agents/research-scanner.md` produced a 5-page survey of 6 candidate repos in ~213s wall-clock + 53K tokens (sonnet, run_in_background=true). Output structured per agent-file template: per-candidate findings with verbatim README quotes + commit SHAs + as-of dates; scoring matrix (5×6, scores cite source); adversarial bear case (4 distinct points); provenance log (18 URL+SHA+as-of rows). Critically, the adversarial scan caught `openai/swarm` is dead since 2025-03 (>400 days, README explicitly redirects to OpenAI Agents SDK) — a candidate that \"minimal orchestration\" framing might have led an unreviewed pick to choose. Scan was the only thing standing between \"shallow seed-list pick\" and \"informed adversarial selection\".\n**Rule**: Every research-scanner dispatch MUST produce: (a) provenance log with repo URL + commit SHA-7 + as-of date for every cited fact; (b) license field per candidate (skip-fast disqualifies AGPL/proprietary if use case is permissive-only); (c) scoring on stockforge-specific axes with each score citing a verbatim README/commit quote (no \"feel\" scoring); (d) ≥3-distinct-point adversarial bear case for the picked candidate (maintenance staleness / license / integration cost / frontier-model substitution); (e) ≥1 disqualifier surfaced for at least one rejected candidate (signal: scan was adversarial, not just confirming). If <3 bear points emerge → assume scan was shallow; lengthen Phase 3-4 fact-gathering, do not ship the report.\n**Anti-example**: A 1-page summary \"DSPy looks great, license MIT, recent commits\" with no SHAs, no quotes, no adversarial section — invoker cannot audit the pick; six months later when DSPy lab graduates and the repo stalls, no one remembers why it was picked over runner-up.\n**Correct example**: `agent-workspace/learning-data/dogfood/agent-pick-1-research-report-20260429T131201Z.md` — 18-row provenance log, scores cite README lines, 4 bear points (frontier substitution / abstraction mismatch / Stanford research governance / LLM-as-judge in MIPRO), `openai/swarm` disqualified with explicit \">400 days since substantive commit + README explicitly redirects\" reason.\n**Severity**: high (a research scan that's not adversarial is an echo chamber for the seed list; AP-1 same-agent-self-review applies — pick-without-bear-case is structurally unfalsifiable).\n**Auto-detect**: yes — Stop hook can grep new `learning-data/dogfood/research-*.md` for required frontmatter (`picked:` + `as_of:`) + section headers (`## Adversarial Bear Case` + `## Provenance Log`); soft-warn if missing. Cheap enforcement; promotion target = Track 5.5d hook extension.","originalFile":null,"structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":224,"oldLines":3,"newStart":224,"newLines":19,"lines":[" **Severity**: medium (recurring spec-drafting risk; Spec-as-source butterfly = drift signal D4 territory)."," **Auto-detect**: partial — drift-detector D4 already scans for spec ↔ code dangling refs. Updated via `promote-rule` skill clusters.\n  Source-grounded — every cell cites a concrete observation (agent-notes entry, charter invariant,\n  drift-log finding, or post-mortem).\nsource_decision: agent-workspace/memory/decisions/003-up06-track-5.5-sync-layer-selfcap.md\nsource_intent: human-workspace/user_prompt/20260429_06.txt §3\nrelated_skills:\n  - .claude/skills/decompose-work/SKILL.md  (consumer)\n  - .claude/skills/promote-rule/SKILL.md     (writer)\n  - .claude/skills/try-n-approaches/SKILL.md  (S9 — consumer)\n---\n\n# Capability Map\n\n> Sparse cells. Append-only by default; cells may transition state with annotation.\n> Per UP-06 §3: \"skill hoặc agent với khả năng chuyên biệt giúp 'bóc tách' deterministic vs llm probability\".\n> This map IS the grounding for that decomposition.\n\n## Dimensions\n\n```yaml\nmodel:      [opus-4.7, sonnet-4.6, haiku-4.5, any]   # \"any\" = model-agnostic (charter / architectural)\neffort:     [low, medium, high, xhigh, max, any]\ntask_class: <see below; appended as new task types observed>\n```\n\n## Task Classes (current vocabulary)\n\n- **math** — numerical computation\n- **synthesis** — multi-source narrative summary\n- **design** — architecture / interface choice\n- **naming** — short identifier creation (variables, files, clusters)\n- **extraction** — claim extraction from unstructured text\n- **classification** — fuzzy categorization (sentiment, drift severity, intent)\n- **calibration** — confidence claim with hit-rate trace\n- **adversarial** — bear/critic/devil's-advocate perspective\n- **verification** — checking measurable properties against claims\n- **qa-composition** — Q&A bundle authoring (incl. charter-tier split)\n- **qa-input-channel** — surfacing questions through right surface (ask vs file)\n- **intent-classification** — routing user prompts to actions\n- **session-bootstrap** — SessionStart hook + first-prompt processing\n- **bash-scripting** — shell scripts (hooks, automation)\n- **adr-authoring** — decision records with provenance + delta summary\n- **thesis-output** — stockforge thesis with bull/bear/calibration\n- **data-reconciliation** — multi-source numeric divergence\n\n---\n\n## Strengths Observed\n\n| # | model | effort | task_class | observation | source |\n|---|---|---|---|---|---|\n| 1 | opus-4.7 | high | design | Multi-mechanism ensemble design (4-mechanism sync infra in 1 plan) lands well. Trade-off matrices natural. | agent-notes 2026-04-29 D-003 R1+R2 + sessions/S6+S7 |\n| 2 | opus-4.7 | high | adversarial | Bear-case generation per thesis is reliable when prompted explicitly with `≥3 distinct points`. | agent-notes 2026-04-23 § Bear Case Required |\n| 3 | opus-4.7 | medium | adr-authoring | 12-field schema ADR with provenance citations consistently produced when template provided. | sessions/S2..S7 D-001..D-004 |\n| 4 | sonnet-4.6 | medium | extraction | Vietnamese KOL transcript claim extraction with structured JSON output is reliable when prompt enforces source_url + extracted_at fields. | evidence-extraction skill (planned Phase 2) + charter Tier 3 |\n| 5 | sonnet-4.6 | low | intent-classification | Trivial-prompt detection (\"ok continue\", \"yes proceed\") cheap + accurate via lite-detect patterns. | agent-notes 2026-04-29 § ok continue idiom + lite-detect-patterns.md |\n| 6 | haiku-4.5 | low | naming | Short identifier naming (cluster titles, variable names) acceptable when context narrowly scoped. | inferred — used for promote-rule cluster naming step |\n| 7 | any | any | qa-composition | Bundle skeleton with `defer_cycle: 0` field at creation prevents stale-bundle drift. | agent-notes 2026-04-29 § Q&A Bundle Skeleton |\n\n---\n\n## Limits Observed\n\n| # | model | effort | task_class | observation | source |\n|---|---|---|---|---|---|\n| L-1 | any | any | math | NO-LLM-math invariant absolute (I-S1). LLM never outputs numbers it computed. | charter principle 9 + agent-notes 2026-04-23 § LLM Never Outputs Numbers |\n| L-2 | any | any | calibration | \"X% confidence\" without n_samples + lookback = drift. Confidence MUST trace to historical hit rate. | charter principle 8 + agent-notes 2026-04-23 § Confidence Requires Calibration Data |\n| L-3 | opus-4.7 | high | verification | False self-attestation on measurable properties (LOC, byte count, presence). Must use `wc -l`, `Glob`, `Grep` to verify; never estimate from memory. | agent-notes 2026-04-29 § AP-S2-3 + S2 drift audit |\n| L-4 | opus-4.7 | high | adversarial | Same-agent self-review = echo chamber (AP-1). Always dispatch fresh-context Verifier. | patterns-discovered AP-1 + sandwich pattern doctrine |\n| L-5 | any | any | qa-input-channel | File-only Q&A with default-after-N-hours = silent absorption. AskUserQuestion is the PRIMARY channel; file = audit only. | UP-04 + UP-06 + agent-notes 2026-04-29 § NO Silent File-Defaults |\n| L-6 | any | any | qa-composition | Charter-tier questions in mixed-tier bundles get absorbed via \"ok continue\" → drift. Charter-tier MUST split into dedicated bundle. | agent-notes 2026-04-29 § Charter-Tier Items + post-audit G1 |\n| L-7 | any | any | session-bootstrap | Stale-prompt references (UP-N closed work) without deterministic detection block autonomous flow. Hook-based detection is the only viable Guardian. | agent-notes 2026-04-29 § Stale-Prompt Reference Check + mistake-log M-S7-1 |\n| L-8 | opus-4.7 | high | adr-authoring | REV-N with ≥10 amendments OR ≥25% budget delta needs explicit \"Delta Summary\" headline; otherwise user absorbs without seeing magnitude. | agent-notes 2026-04-29 § Pre-Amendment Delta Summary |\n| L-9 | any | any | bash-scripting | `grep -c \\|\\| echo 0` pattern double-counts zero on no-match. Use `\\|\\| true` + regex validate instead. | agent-notes 2026-04-29 S3 § grep -c |\n| L-10 | any | any | data-reconciliation | Multi-source numeric divergence (vnstock vs FiinPro) silently averaged = drift. Output divergence explicitly with provenance. | agent-notes 2026-04-23 § Source Provider Disagreement |\n| L-11 | any | any | thesis-output | Single-perspective thesis = anti-pattern. Bear case ≥3 distinct points mandatory; refuse render without it. | charter principle 3 + agent-notes 2026-04-23 § Bear Case Required |\n| L-12 | any | any | session-bootstrap | Continue-injector unconditional in SUPERVISED = race condition with user typing. MUST gate by autonomous_mode flag. | agent-notes 2026-04-29 § Continue-Injector Gated + mistake-log M-S7-1 L2 |\n\n---\n\n## Heuristics for Decompose-Work (consumer)\n\nWhen `decompose-work` skill classifies a sub-task:\n\n1. **Cite this map**: which strength row supports \"do via LLM\"? Which limit row forces \"do deterministic\"?\n2. **Default to deterministic on conflict**: if a row is in *both* strengths AND limits → safer to assume LIMIT (under-promise).\n3. **Map gap → propose entry**: if task_class is novel (not in vocabulary above), add tentative entry post-execution + flag `provisional: true`.\n4. **Effort-tier matters**: a task_class strength at `effort=max` may be a limit at `effort=low`. Default to medium if unsure.\n\n## Promotion Path (writer = promote-rule skill)\n\nWhen `promote-rule` clusters agent-notes:\n- Cluster naming → adds new task_class to vocabulary (if novel)\n- Cluster of ≥3 limits with same task_class → likely capability-map limit row\n- Cluster of ≥3 strengths with same model+effort+task_class → strength row\n- Single-occurrence observations → DO NOT add to map (one-off, may not generalize)\n\n## Update Protocol\n\n- **From agent-notes via promote-rule** (S8+): `promote-rule` skill scans agent-notes; clusters of ≥3 → proposes capability-map entry. Agent reviews + writes.\n- **From drift-log**: new limit observed → append to Limits Observed with source citation.\n- **From thesis post-mortem** (Phase 1+): observed strength/limit per stock-domain task_class → append.\n- **State transition**: if a previously-strength row becomes a limit (regression), mark old row `superseded-by: row-N` and add new row.\n\n## Anti-Patterns\n\n- Adding capability-map entries from speculation, not observation. Every cell cites a source.\n- Single-occurrence \"X failed once\" → not a limit yet. Wait for ≥3 instances.\n- Generalizing model-specific findings to `any` → keep model-tagged unless invariant.\n- Skipping `effort` dimension → \"opus is good at design\" without effort tier loses signal.\n- Treating limits as permanent → revisit on model upgrade (Opus 4.7 → 4.8 → 5.0 may invalidate).\n\n## Notes\n\n- Counts: 7 strengths + 12 limits = 19 total entries (≥10 S8 target ✓).\n- Skill capability claim is a future state — try-n-approaches skill (S9) will explicitly track approach×model×task_class outcomes; that data flows here.\n- For stockforge stock-domain task classes (thesis synthesis, KOL extraction, pump detection) — most cells assumed-aligned until Phase 1+ dogfood data accumulates.\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":48,"oldLines":6,"newStart":48,"newLines":7,"lines":[" - **adr-authoring** — decision records with provenance + delta summary"," - **thesis-output** — stockforge thesis with bull/bear/calibration"," - **data-reconciliation** — multi-source numeric divergence","+- **tool-survey** _(provisional, S12 first instance)_ — opensource-repo fitness scan with provenance + adversarial bear case + license capture; produced via `.claude/agents/research-scanner.md` dispatch. Strength/limit rows deferred until ≥3 instances per promotion threshold."," "," ---"," "]}],"userModified":false,"replaceAll":false},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"7f6e40b5-8bef-48f6-b63f-352f2b98bf41","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"45ff2d8f-d633-45f8-8a6e-c5fb5ce08842","version":"2.1.122","gitBranch":"main"}

≤5K over due to multi-WebFetch survey of 6 repos)\n- **Tool uses**: 30\n- **Output**: 322-LOC report at `agent-workspace/learning-data/dogfood/agent-pick-1-research-report-20260429T131201Z.md`\n- **Picked**: `stanfordnlp/dspy@db83e5ad` (MIT, 1 day since last commit, healthy)\n- **Surprise finding** (per dispatcher return): `openai/swarm` is ABANDONWARE since 2025-03 (>400d), README explicitly redirects to OpenAI Agents SDK — would have been a dead-end pick without adversarial scan.\n\n---\n\n## IMPL-tier decisions made (per D-003 § Open Questions doctrine)\n\n### IMPL-S12-1: Research-scanner output path = `dogfood/<TS>.md` not `dogfood/<picked-tool>.md`\n\n**Discovery**: D-005 § 5.5d.3 prose says output goes to `dogfood/<picked-tool>.md`. But the research-scanner methodology produces a SURVEY of multiple candidates BEFORE picking — naming the file after the picked tool means the file name encodes the conclusion, which: (a) prevents writing the file until the conclusion is reached (artificial sequencing); (b) hides what was rejected from filename-only inspection; (c) confuses two artifacts (survey-report ≠ dogfood-insight) into one path.\n\n**Decision**: split into TWO artifacts.\n- Survey report: `dogfood/agent-pick-1-research-report-<TS>.md` — what the research-scanner produces (multi-candidate; conclusion at top, evidence below).\n- Dogfood insight: `dogfood/<picked-tool>.md` — what the dogfood loop produces (single-tool focused; what we LEARN from the picked tool to apply to stockforge).\n\n**Rationale**: matches DSPy's own discipline of separating evaluation (multi-input) from compilation (focused improvement). Each artifact has one job. Filename inspection of `dogfood/` directory shows both the survey log AND the conclusions over time as separate files; future `dogfood/langgraph.md` (hypothetical) would sit alongside `dogfood/dspy.md` cleanly.\n\n**Trade-off accepted**: 1 extra file in `learning-data/dogfood/` per dogfood cycle (vs prose's implied 1 file). For 6-month projection of ~6-12 dogfood cycles, +6-12 files is negligible vs filename-clarity gain.\n\n**D-005 amendment carry-over**: surface in S15 (Track 7) D-005 amendment alongside IMPL-S11-2 path-resolution carry-over. Append-only.\n\n### IMPL-S12-2: research-scanner.md tools = read-only set (no Bash, no Write)\n\n**Discovery**: research-scanner's job is to fetch external content (READMEs + commits via WebFetch) and produce a report. Two tooling options were evaluated: (A) include Bash for `git ls-remote` style SHA lookups; (B) WebFetch-only.\n\n**Decision**: WebFetch-only. Tools list = `[Read, Glob, Grep, WebFetch]`. No Bash. No Edit. No Write (the dispatched agent itself writes via its general-purpose Write inheritance, but the SUBAGENT TEMPLATE only declares the read-research surface).\n\n**Rationale**: minimum risk surface. Bash in research-scanner adds clone/install/exec attack-surface that is irrelevant to the read-only research role. WebFetch is purpose-fit. (The dispatched general-purpose agent inherits its full `*` toolset — but our research-scanner.md declares the methodology, not the tool grants; declaring the read-only surface enforces the discipline at the methodology level even when the actual dispatch model has broader access.)\n\n**Trade-off accepted**: agent must use github.com URLs (`raw.githubusercontent.com/.../HEAD/README.md` + `api.github.com/repos/.../commits`) rather than git protocol. Slightly less SHA-precision (HEAD ≠ specific tag), but more portable + Phase-0 friendly + no auth needed.\n\n### IMPL-S12-3: Capability-map task class addition = vocabulary only, no strength row\n\n**Discovery**: D-005 § 5.5d.3 success criterion #4 mentioned \"capability-map.md += possibly +1 task_class cell (e.g., `tool-survey`)\". Capability-map's own promotion rules say: \"Single-occurrence observations → DO NOT add to map (one-off, may not generalize)\" but ALSO \"Map gap → propose entry: if task_class is novel, add tentative entry post-execution + flag `provisional: true`\".\n\n**Decision**: append `tool-survey` to vocabulary list (line ~50) WITH `_(provisional, S12 first instance)_` flag. Do NOT add a strength row in the table (waits for ≥3 instances per promotion threshold).\n\n**Rationale**: vocabulary expansion is permitted on novel observation per Heuristic #3; row addition (strengths/limits table) requires ≥3 instances per \"Update Protocol\" line. Splitting the rules respects both: vocabulary captures the new concept's existence; row-data waits for evidence of generalizability.\n\n---\n\n## Lessons learned (3 candidates; promotion handled in agent-notes)\n\n### L-S12-1: Self-learning claims need deterministic metric function (already promoted to agent-notes ≥1)\n\n**Promoted**: agent-notes.md § \"2026-04-29 (S12 / Track 5.5d.3 dogfood): Self-Learning / Outer-Loop Claims Require Deterministic Metric Function\" (severity: high; auto-detect: yes via Stop hook ext on `loop/*-experiment-frame.md` missing `metric_function:` field).\n\n### L-S12-2: Research-scanner dispatch discipline (already promoted to agent-notes ≥2)\n\n**Promoted**: agent-notes.md § \"2026-04-29 (S12 / Track 5.5d.3 dogfood): Research-Scanner Dispatch Discipline — Provenance + Adversarial + License Required\" (severity: high; auto-detect: yes via grep on dogfood reports for required frontmatter + section headers).\n\n### L-S12-3: Survey vs Dogfood-Insight file separation (IMPL-tier; documented in IMPL-S12-1)\n\n**Status**: not promoted to agent-notes yet (single-instance; surfaces if recurring). But the research-scanner methodology produces a SURVEY of multiple candidates BEFORE picking — naming the file after the picked tool means the file name encodes the conclusion, which: (a) prevents writing the file until the conclusion is reached (artificial sequencing); (b) hides what was rejected from filename-only inspection; (c) confuses two artifacts (survey-report ≠ dogfood-insight) into one path.\n\n**Decision**: split into TWO artifacts.\n- Survey report: `dogfood/agent-pick-1-research-report-<TS>.md` — what the research-scanner produces (multi-candidate; conclusion at top, evidence below).\n- Dogfood insight: `dogfood/<picked-tool>.md` — what the dogfood loop produces (single-tool focused; what we LEARN from the picked tool to apply to stockforge).\n\n**Rationale**: matches DSPy's own discipline of separating evaluation (multi-input) from compilation (focused improvement). Each artifact has one job. Filename inspection of `dogfood/` directory shows both the survey log AND the conclusions over time as separate files; future `dogfood/langgraph.md` (hypothetical) would sit alongside `dogfood/dspy.md` cleanly.\n\n**Trade-off accepted**: 1 extra file in `learning-data/dogfood/` per dogfood cycle (vs prose's implied 1 file). For 6-month projection of ~6-12 dogfood cycles, +6-12 files is negligible vs filename-clarity gain.\n\n**D-005 amendment carry-over**: surface in S15 (Track 7) D-005 amendment alongside IMPL-S11-2 path-resolution carry-over. Append-only.\n\n### IMPL-S12-2: research-scanner.md tools = read-only set (no Bash, no Write)\n\n**Discovery**: research-scanner's job is to fetch external content (READMEs + commits via WebFetch) and produce a report. Two tooling options were evaluated: (A) include Bash for `git ls-remote` style SHA lookups; (B) WebFetch-only.\n\n**Decision**: WebFetch-only. Tools list = `[Read, Glob, Grep, WebFetch]`. No Bash. No Edit. No Write (the dispatched agent itself writes via its general-purpose Write inheritance, but the SUBAGENT TEMPLATE only declares the read-research surface).\n\n**Rationale**: minimum risk surface. Bash in research-scanner adds clone/install/exec attack-surface that is irrelevant to the read-only research role. WebFetch is purpose-fit. (The dispatched general-purpose agent inherits its full `*` toolset — but our research-scanner.md declares the methodology, not the tool grants; declaring the read-only surface enforces the discipline at the methodology level even when the actual dispatch model has broader access.)\n\n**Trade-off accepted**: agent must use github.com URLs (`raw.githubusercontent.com/.../HEAD/README.md` + `api.github.com/repos/.../commits`) rather than git protocol. Slightly less SHA-precision (HEAD ≠ specific tag), but more portable + Phase-0 friendly + no auth needed.\n\n### IMPL-S12-3: Capability-map task class addition = vocabulary only, no strength row\n\n**Discovery**: D-005 § 5.5d.3 success criterion #4 mentioned \"capability-map.md += possibly +1 task_class cell (e.g., `tool-survey`)\". Capability-map's own promotion rules say: \"Single-occurrence observations → DO NOT add to map (one-off, may not generalize)\" but ALSO \"Map gap → propose entry: if task_class is novel, add tentative entry post-execution + flag `provisional: true`\".\n\n**Decision**: append `tool-survey` to vocabulary list (line ~50) WITH `_(provisional, S12 first instance)_` flag. Do NOT add a strength row in the table (waits for ≥3 instances per promotion threshold).\n\n**Rationale**: vocabulary expansion is permitted on novel observation per Heuristic #3; row addition (strengths/limits table) requires ≥3 instances per \"Update Protocol\" line. Splitting the rules respects both: vocabulary captures the new concept's existence; row-data waits for evidence of generalizability.\n\n---\n\n## Lessons learned (3 candidates; promotion handled in agent-notes)\n\n### L-S12-1: Self-learning claims need deterministic metric function (already promoted to agent-notes ≥1)\n\n**Promoted**: agent-notes.md § \"2026-04-29 (S12 / Track 5.5d.3 dogfood): Self-Learning / Outer-Loop Claims Require Deterministic Metric Function\" (severity: high; auto-detect: yes via Stop hook ext on `loop/*-experiment-frame.md` missing `metric_function:` field).\n\n### L-S12-2: Research-scanner dispatch discipline (already promoted to agent-notes ≥2)\n\n**Promoted**: agent-notes.md § \"2026-04-29 (S12 / Track 5.5d.3 dogfood): Research-Scanner Dispatch Discipline — Provenance + Adversarial + License Required\" (severity: high; auto-detect: yes via grep on dogfood reports for required frontmatter + section headers).\n\n### L-S12-3: Survey vs Dogfood-Insight file separation (IMPL-tier; documented in IMPL-S12-1)\n\n**Status**: not promoted to agent-notes yet (single-instance; surfaces if recurring). Documented as IMPL-S12-1 in this session log + carry-over for D-005 amendment at S15 Track 7 (paired with IMPL-S11-2).\n\n---\n\n## Drift-Watch (S12 close)\n\n- **DR1 (LOC ceiling)**: 24 violations unchanged from S11. **0 NEW** from S12 (research-scanner.md = 174 < 200 ceiling; learning-data/* artifacts not under D1 ceiling).\n- **DR2 (self-attestation)**: 2 pre-existing violations.
